Result: 研究尚在进行中，目前已完成了初步的开发者访谈并据此调整了方案，计划进行更多访谈和A/B测试来验证方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过结构化增强bug报告模板，有望实现更有效和高效的bug分诊与解决，但最终结论需要后续访谈和A/B测试的验证。

Abstract: We propose enhancing the bug report templates in the GitHub Issues issue tracking system used by the TianoCore open-source community with the aim of improving the bug triage and resolution process. We analyze the bug repository data and find patterns of information that are useful for bug triage and fixing. However, some of them are only occasionally included in the free-form text of bug reports. Therefore, we propose adding a few new fields to the existing TianoCore bug report template. In this study, we focus on the key TianoCore project, EDK II, which constitutes the core of the UEFI firmware across various firmware vendors and original equipment manufacturers. This study is currently a work-in-progress. So far, we have interviewed a few developers to obtain their feedback and adjust the proposed approach. We are planning more interviews with the TianoCore community to conduct A/B tests and validate our approach to achieve effective and efficient bug triage and resolution.

</details>


### [100] [Automated Synthesis of Cloud Emulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23842)
*Archit Bhatnagar,Zhenning Yang,Sarah McClure,Yiming Qiu,Sylvia Ratnasamy,Ang Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CloudEmu是一种基于云文档自动构建云模拟器的神经符号代码合成方法，结合LLM的代码生成能力与云特定的符号抽象，以真实云作为验证引擎，在AWS和GCP上优于人工开发十余年的LocalStack工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DevOps程序测试需要在真实云资源上执行，耗时、不安全且成本高昂。现有云模拟器依赖开发者手动解读大量云文档并手工构建每个服务、API及其交互逻辑，难以应对云服务的复杂性和快速演进，缺乏可扩展的自动化方案。

Method: CloudEmu采用神经符号代码合成方法，利用LLM在文档理解和代码生成方面的能力，结合云特定的符号抽象来抑制幻觉并保证规模化精度；同时使用真实云作为oracle进行自动化测试、修复和对齐。

Result: 在AWS和GCP主要云服务上的评估显示，CloudEmu在覆盖率和准确性方面均表现优异，超越了由大型工程师团队手工开发十余年的现有领先工具LocalStack。

Conclusion: CloudEmu证明基于文档自动合成云模拟器是可行且高效的，能够以自动化方式替代大量的手工工程投入，为云计算基础设施测试提供可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: DevOps programming (e.g., using CLI/API scripts or IaC frameworks) is key to cloud infrastructure management. Unlike traditional programming tasks, DevOps program testing needs provisioning and execution against actual cloud resources, which is often time-consuming, unsafe, and costly. Cloud emulators have gained popularity for easing DevOps program testing; they are generally API-level mocks that can execute DevOps programs in a local environment. Still, building these emulators remains challenging: developers must manually interpret extensive cloud documentation and handcraft logic for each service, API, and their interaction. This does not scale to the complexity of the cloud, which is further a moving target as the services and APIs evolve. CloudEmu is an automated approach that constructs emulators based on cloud documentation via neurosymbolic code synthesis. The key idea is to combine LLMs' general strengths in documentation understanding and code generation with cloud-specific symbolic abstractions that suppress hallucinations and enforce precision at scale, while using the real cloud as an oracle for automated testing, repair, and alignment. Our evaluation shows the effectiveness of CloudEmu on major cloud provider (AWS and GCP) services in both coverage and accuracy. CloudEmu outperforms the existing leading tool LocalStack, which was manually developed by a large team of engineers over a decade.

</details>


### [101] [SPIDER4TianoCore: Enhancing Patch-Propagation for the TianoCore UEFI Firmware Development Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23755)
*Laura Baird,Devin Haggitt,Terrance E. Boult,Aravind Machiry,Armin Moin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出并演示了SPIDER4TianoCore，一个Python命令行工具，为TianoCore/UEFI固件供应链提供集成阶段的补丁状态证据，可将上游补丁对与下游目标分类为Vulnerable、Already Patched、Not Applicable或Uncertain。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TianoCore/UEFI固件供应链中，下游仓库需要判断上游安全补丁是否已应用或适用，但目前缺乏自动化工具来提供补丁传播状态的可复现证据，帮助维护者进行审查决策。

Method: 基于SPIDER的补丁传播框架思想，构建了一个命令行工具，输入上游补丁前后对和准备好的下游目标，通过分析器输出带有证据的分类结果（Vulnerable、Already Patched、Not Applicable或Uncertain），供维护者人工审查。

Result: 在来自八个公共下游EDK II仓库、两个CVE的20个目标/CVE对上进行评估：分析器产生10个高置信度补丁前匹配和4个高置信度补丁后匹配，对6个目标保守弃权，没有出现相对人工记录的补丁状态标签的自信错误分类。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明该工具能为准备好的目标生成可复现的证据，但仅代表初步验证而非通用下游准确性；工具本身并不证明补丁安全可传播，仅提供状态证据辅助维护者审查。

Abstract: We propose and demonstrate SPIDER4TianoCore, a packaged Python command-line tool that provides integration-stage patch-status evidence for the TianoCore/UEFI firmware supply chain. Given an upstream pre-patch and post-patch pair and prepared downstream targets, the tool reports Vulnerable, Already Patched, Not Applicable, or Uncertain with supporting evidence for maintainer review. Our work is inspired by SPIDER's patch-propagation framing, but SPIDER4TianoCore does not itself prove that a patch is safe to propagate. We evaluate the engine on 20 prepared target/CVE pairs from eight public downstream EDK II repositories and two CVEs. The analyzers produce 10 high-confidence pre-patch matches and four high-confidence post-patch matches, conservatively abstain on six targets, and make no confidently wrong classifications relative to the recorded manual patch-state labels. These preliminary results demonstrate reproducible evidence generation for prepared targets rather than general downstream accuracy.

</details>


### [102] [Scale, Concentration, and Entry Timing in the Shopify App Ecosystem: A Longitudinal Study of Platform Governance and Application Survival](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23771)
*Fabrizio Assabese,Peter-Jan Celis,Giuseppe Destefanis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于2025年9月的快照数据、366周的面板数据及互联网档案馆的上市历史记录，系统分析了Shopify应用市场的结构、驱动因素与平台治理效应。研究发现，多数应用类别竞争充分，后进入者增速快于先行者；平台自身的进入和退出会不对称地重塑竞争格局；应用失败具有高度可预测性，且市场份额领导权频繁更替。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shopify市场拥有超过16,000个活跃第三方应用和270万商家店铺，年销售额估计达7060亿美元，但缺乏对其市场结构和采用驱动因素的实证研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供关于平台应用生态系统的系统性经验证据。

Method: 使用多源数据：（1）2025年9月全部24,826个应用快照；（2）7,708个应用的366周面板数据（2019年2月至2026年3月）；（3）通过互联网档案馆重构的上架历史。分析涵盖55个功能类别的集中度、进入顺序效应、平台治理干预影响，以及应用退出预测模型。

Result: 主要发现：55个类别中超半数低集中度、20%高度集中；88%的类别中后进入者增长快于先行者；Shopify进入聊天领域逆转了两年多的去集中化趋势；其弃用Product Reviews应用释放了191,000个安装；2021年平台收入分成降低未产生明显影响；应用退出高度可预测（AUC>0.8），92%类别的领导权在七年内易主。

Conclusion: Shopify应用市场保持可竞争性，市场受益者更多取决于平台治理和进入条件而非进入时机。该研究首次量化了平台所有者退出互补者类别的效应，为平台生态治理提供了重要实证依据。

Abstract: The Shopify marketplace hosts more than 16,000 active third-party applications, serving 2.7 million active merchant stores generating an estimated \$706 billion in annual sales, yet little empirical evidence exists on its structure and adoption drivers. We analyse it using a September 2025 snapshot of all 24,826 applications, a weekly panel of 7,708 applications over 366 weeks (February 2019 to March 2026), and listing histories reconstructed from the Internet Archive. Across 55 established functional categories, over half were low-concentration and 20\% highly concentrated, with larger categories consistently less concentrated. Later entrants grew faster than early movers in 88\% of the 50 analysed categories, persisting across seven years of quarterly re-estimations, with early movers on net losing detected installations whilst late movers gained them. Platform governance reshaped competition asymmetrically: Shopify's entry into chat reversed more than two years of de-concentration; the deprecation of its Product Reviews application, to our knowledge the first measured platform-owner exit from complementor category, released 191,000 installations of which at most a third reappeared as competitor adoption within a year; the 2021 reduction of the platform revenue share produced no detectable change in entry or retention. Failure is largely silent and predictable: the median exiting application peaked at 8 detected installations and disappeared from tracking within 68 weeks of launch, category leadership changed hands in 92\% of categories over seven years, and publicly observable data from an application's first six months predict exit within two years with cross-validated AUC above 0.8. App markets on Shopify remain contestable; who benefits depends on platform governance and entry conditions more than on entry timing.

</details>


### [103] [The Empire, Long Divided, Must Unite: Architectural Convergence in Three LLM Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23953)
*Dai Jiahong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对三个开源编码智能体框架（deepagents、pi、dsh）的源码级多案例研究，发现两个成熟的框架尽管初始哲学对立，却在架构上趋同于五种共性元素（商品化循环、可追加回放会话记录、模型怪癖作为数据处理、渐进式上下文披露、显式扩展缝）；同时指出"外部可验证性"这一维度在现有框架中完全缺失，预测这是未来溯源敏感领域智能体框架竞争的下一个关键轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体框架（agent harness）——围绕语言模型的周边代码（构建上下文、编排工具、运行循环、持久化状态）——日益成为约束智能体行为的关键瓶颈，而非模型本身。作者希望通过系统研究开源框架，理解这一关键层如何演化、存在哪些共性设计，以及缺失什么关键能力。

Method: 采用源码级多案例研究方法：选取三个哲学立场迥异的开源编码智能体框架进行深入分析——LangChain的deepagents（"开箱即用"）、Earendil的pi（极端极简主义）、DeepSeek的dsh（"一切皆插件"）。对每个框架固定在特定commit上细读，并追踪其提交历史，分析设计演变轨迹；最后一个框架作为留出（held-out）验证样本。

Result: 发现：两个成熟框架（deepagents和pi）在演化方向上背道而驰（deepagents在削减人工脚手架，pi在积累持久化基础设施），却收敛到同一架构中间形态，包含五个反复出现的结构元素：商品化的主循环、可追加且可回放的会话记录、将模型怪癖数据处理化、上下文渐进式披露、显式扩展缝。第三个框架（dsh）作为留出验证，表现出全部五种元素，甚至在某个扩展缝中直接复用了另一框架的实现。收敛可分解为并行发现、扩散传播和字面复用三种机制。此外，"外部可验证性"（一种无需信任运行时可被外部查验的防篡改记录）在所有框架中完全缺失。

Conclusion: 智能体框架架构的收敛表明存在一种"自然选择"出的中间设计形态；而外部可验证性的缺失并非疏忽，而是一个"预测性空白"——对于溯源敏感（provenance-sensitive）领域的应用，可验证性将成为下一代智能体框架差异化竞争的新轴线。

Abstract: An agent harness is what turns a language model into an autonomous agent: the surrounding code that builds the model's context, mediates its tools, runs the loop, and persists state across a long-horizon run. This layer, not the model it wraps, is increasingly the binding constraint on agent behaviour. We present a source-level, multi-case study of three open coding-agent harnesses built from deliberately opposing philosophies: LangChain's deepagents (batteries-included), Earendil's pi (radical minimalism), and DeepSeek's dsh (everything-is-a-plugin). Reading each at a pinned commit and following its commit history, we find that the two mature harnesses have travelled in opposite directions (deepagents subtracting authored scaffolding, pi accreting durable infrastructure), yet converged toward one architectural middle form of five recurring elements: a commoditised loop, an append-only replayable session record, model quirks kept as data, progressive disclosure of context, and explicit extension seams. A third harness, read afterward as a held-out check, exhibits all five, and in one seam reuses another's implementation outright. We therefore do not claim independent invention, and decompose the convergence into parallel discovery, diffusion, and literal reuse. Finally, one load-bearing dimension shows no convergence, and indeed no presence: external verifiability, a tamper-evident record an outside party can check without trusting the runtime. We read this absence not as an oversight but as a predictive gap, the next axis on which harnesses for provenance-sensitive domains will differ.

</details>


### [104] [Evaluating Language Models on Cross-Language Code Functional Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23961)
*Hui Sun,Anderson Uchôa,Rohit Gheyi,Wesley K. G. Assunção*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了PolyHuman数据集，用于评估LLM在跨编程语言（CPP、Java、Python）的人类编写代码中判断功能等价性的能力。研究发现LLM存在难度依赖性的误判、语言敏感性差异以及依赖表面相似性线索的问题，表明当前LLM并不能可靠地捕获跨语言的功能等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码理解评估主要集中于单语言环境或合成代码，无法真实反映模型对程序语义的深层理解。作者希望在人写的跨语言代码场景中检验LLM判断功能等价性的能力，以揭示模型是真正理解语义还是依赖表面相似性。

Method: 构建PolyHuman数据集（包含CPP、Java、Python的人写程序），评估多种开源和专有LLM在语内和跨语言等价性检测上的表现；选取GPT-o4-mini为代表性模型验证稳定性；人工分析81个系统性错误案例，并结合Chain-of-Thought推理进行归类；最后跨GPT-o4-mini、Claude-Opus-4.7和Gemini-3-Flash对比，判断错误是否具有模型特异性。

Result: 发现等价性判断存在难度依赖性退化（困难问题更容易把非等价代码误判为等价）；最佳模型对编程语言存在敏感性（尤其对Python更保守）；模型部分依赖相似性线索；GPT-o4-mini在相同设置下存在显著的运行间不稳定性，表明能力不稳定而非完全缺失。

Conclusion: 当前LLM无论在语内还是跨语言场景下，均不能可靠地捕获程序的功能等价性，其表面性能可能部分源于浅层启发式而非真实的语义推理能力。

Abstract: Background: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance across a variety of code-understanding tasks, leading many to believe that they can reason about program semantics. However, existing evaluations primarily focus on single-language settings or rely on synthetically generated code, raising concerns about whether current results reflect true semantic understanding. Aims: We investigate whether LLMs can accurately judge functional equivalence across different programming languages in human-written code, a setting that requires deeper reasoning beyond superficial similarity. Method: We introduce PolyHuman, a dataset of human-written programs in CPP, Java, and Python. Using this dataset, we evaluate intra- and inter-language equivalence detection across open-weight and proprietary LLMs, selecting GPT-o4-mini as a representative model to assess stability. We then manually analyze 81 cases of systematic disagreement in which models incorrectly judge functional equivalence, examining the code logic and the generated Chain-of-Thought reasoning. Finally, we categorize these failures and compare them across GPT-o4-mini, Claude-Opus-4.7, and Gemini-3-Flash to determine whether they reflect model-specific issues or broader limitations of state-of-the-art LLMs. Results: We identify a difficulty-dependent breakdown in equivalence judgment (harder problems make the model increasingly prone to misclassifying non-equivalent code as equivalent), a model-specific sensitivity to programming language for the best-performing model (particularly a more conservative behavior on Python), and a partial reliance on similarity-based cues. GPT-o4-mini also shows substantial run-to-run instability under identical settings, indicating inconsistent rather than absent capability. Conclusions: Current LLMs do not reliably capture functional equivalence within or across languages.

</details>


### [105] [DeepRepoQA: Code Repository Question Answering with Deep Agent Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24221)
*Weihan Peng,Yuling Shi,Yingwei Ma,Longfei Yun,Beijun Shen,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepRepoQA，一个基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的智能体框架，用于仓库级代码理解问答，通过在SWE-QA基准上的实验证明了其在多跳代码推理中的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库理解方法主要依赖表面级别的代码检索，缺乏跨多文件、复杂软件架构的深度推理能力，也无法将答案扎根于长距离代码依赖关系中。

Method: DeepRepoQA采用智能体框架，通过MCTS机制对仓库结构进行系统性树搜索，使LLM智能体能够动态搜索、导航和检查代码，实现跨长距离代码依赖的多跳推理。

Result: 在SWE-QA基准上的综合实验表明，DeepRepoQA相比强基线方法取得了显著的性能提升，验证了MCTS引导的系统性探索在多跳仓库推理中的有效性。

Conclusion: 系统性的MCTS引导探索是解决仓库级代码深度理解问答的有效途径，能够弥补现有方法在跨文件多跳推理方面的不足。

Abstract: Answering developer questions about a software repository is a critical yet under-explored problem in software engineering. While existing repository understanding methods have advanced the field, they predominantly rely on surface-level code retrieval and lack the ability for deep reasoning over multiple files, complex software architectures, and grounding answers in long-range code dependencies. To address these limitations, we propose DeepRepoQA, a novel question answering (QA) framework for repository-level code understanding. DeepRepoQA builds on an agentic framework where LLM agents find answers through a systematic tree search over the repository structure. A Monte-Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) mechanism is employed to empower agents to dynamically search, navigate, and inspect code, enabling effective multi-hop reasoning over long-range code dependencies. Comprehensive experiments on the SWE-QA benchmark demonstrate substantial performance gains over strong baselines, validating the effectiveness of systematic MCTS-guided exploration for multi-hop repository reasoning.

</details>


### [106] [Mutation Testing of Simulink Cyber-Physical System Models: Challenges and Solutions in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24250)
*Murat Kavak,Onur Kilincceker,Serge Demeyer,Kevin Vandenbroucke,Halim Abdurrahman Ceylan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文分析了在DUCO公司（一家生产建筑通风系统的公司）将变异测试应用于Simulink和Stateflow设计模型的工业实践中遇到的挑战和经验教训，重点发现了等价变异体、需求可追溯性以及Stateflow变异测试这三个学术界尚未充分报告的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变异测试有助于支持左移测试策略，尤其在延迟发现bug成本极高的行业（如网络物理系统）中。然而在工业环境中将变异测试应用于设计模型仍缺乏实际经验，需要探索其落地的挑战。

Method: 在DUCO公司与工程师合作开展试点项目，使用工具原型MUT4SLX对基于Simulink和StateFlow的设计模型进行变异测试，记录和分析实施过程中遇到的问题和经验。

Result: 识别出三个学术文献中尚未充分报告的特定挑战：等价变异体问题、需求可追溯性问题以及Stateflow变异测试的特殊难点。

Conclusion: 将变异测试引入工业模型驱动工程环境面临独特挑战，这些发现对变异测试在左移测试策略中的应用提供了有价值的工业实践见解。

Abstract: Several experience reports illustrate that mutation testing is capable of supporting a shift-left testing strategy, especially in industries where late bug discovery incurs very high costs and risks. In the context of cyber-physical systems, a shift left implies that engineers need to test the design models used to simulate, prototype, and analyse the feasibility of the system under design. In this paper, we analyse the challenges we encountered and the lessons we learned when incorporating mutation testing in the context of DUCO, a company producing ventilation systems for buildings. The engineers within DUCO have years of experience with model-based engineering centered around Simulink and StateFlow, including unit tests for their models. During a pilot project with our tool prototype MUT4SLX we learned that equivalent mutants, requirement traceability, and mutation testing for Stateflow represent particular challenges not yet reported in the academic literature.

</details>


### [107] [Cross-Stack Validation of Language-Model Training: A Clinical Fine-Tuning Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24267)
*Thang Tran,Lan Dang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了轨迹级差分测试协议，用独立实现的训练堆栈（PyTorch vs Zig编写的numbat框架）作为整个微调流程的差分预言机，在Qwen3-0.6B的LoRA微调中发现了17个缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络训练存在"预言机问题"：训练可以正常收敛但底层软件计算错误。现有差分测试仅针对算子和推理路径，缺乏对完整训练管线的独立验证手段。

Method: 定义轨迹级协议：共享规范、跨检查点（涵盖算术、模型加载、数据渲染和学习轨迹）、堆栈/编排/语言运行时的独立性分离。在PyTorch和Zig编写的numbat框架上对Qwen3-0.6B进行LoRA微调，覆盖168,574对临床问答数据，通过numbat的C接口从6种语言驱动。

Result: 42次配对评估（覆盖完整epoch）中，两个堆栈的留出交叉熵平均差异仅0.134%，4种实现最终差异在0.15%以内。发现17个单实现开发遗漏的缺陷，其中影响最大的缺陷（临床文本渲染不匹配）对留出损失的影响比其他算术缺陷大约500倍；4个缺陷仅在内存模型不同的语言中可达。

Conclusion: 独立实现堆栈可作为完整微调管线的有效差分预言机；实现多样性的多个轴中，语言运行时是关键但常被忽视的一维。

Abstract: Neural network training has an oracle problem: a run can converge normally and yield a usable model while the software beneath it computes something other than specified. Almost all such work runs on one stack, so there is rarely anything independent to check against. We study whether independently implemented training stacks can serve as differential oracles for a whole fine-tuning pipeline, rather than the operators and inference paths that prior differential testing targets. We define a trajectory-level protocol -- a shared specification, cross-check points spanning arithmetic, model loading, data rendering and the learning trajectory, and a separation of independence of the stack, the orchestration and the language runtime -- and apply it to a LoRA adaptation of Qwen3-0.6B over 168,574 clinical question-answer pairs under PyTorch and under numbat, an independent framework written in Zig, driven natively and through its C interface from six languages. Across 42 paired evaluations spanning a full epoch the two stacks' held-out cross-entropy differs by 0.134% on average, and four implementations end the epoch within 0.15% of one another. The comparison exposed 17 faults that single-implementation development had missed, two of them notable for software engineering. The fault with the largest effect on the trained model lay outside the numerical kernels: a mismatch in how clinical text was rendered moved held-out loss 0.15, some 500 times more than the arithmetic faults found beside it. And four faults were reachable only from a language whose memory model differs from the first two implementations: a scheduler migrating work across threads, a collector blind to device memory, an ownership discipline needing a primitive the interface lacked. Implementation diversity has several axes, and the runtime is one.

</details>


### [108] [Towards LLM-Enhanced Android Taint Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24269)
*Nicholas Miazzo,Marco Alecci,Jordan Samhi,Jacques Klein,Eleonora Losiouk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨使用现成的大语言模型（LLMs）通过代理式交互策略来推理Android应用中的污点分析问题。初步评估表明，LLM方法在DroidBench基准上显著优于FlowDroid（F1分数0.96 vs 0.55），在FlowDroid薄弱领域如组件间通信、隐式流和反射方面表现尤为突出，并能在真实应用中识别额外潜在数据泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的静态污点分析工具（如FlowDroid）在准确建模Android框架时面临困难，导致在组件间通信、隐式流和反射等复杂场景下表现不佳。作者希望探索是否能利用LLM的能力来弥补这些缺陷。

Method: 采用代理式交互策略（agentic interaction strategy），让LLM迭代探索代码并对数据流进行推理。通过在DroidBench基准上与FlowDroid对比评估，并在少量真实世界应用上进行验证。

Result: Gemini-3 Flash在DroidBench上F1分数达到0.96，远超FlowDroid的0.55；在组件间通信（0.95 vs 0.17）、隐式流（0.94 vs 0.00）和反射（1.00 vs 0.50）等FlowDroid薄弱类别中优势显著；在真实应用中发现了FlowDroid未报告的数据泄露。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明LLM推理可以有效补充传统静态污点分析，为未来研究混合式LLM增强污点分析流水线提供了动机。

Abstract: Taint analysis is a fundamental technique for detecting sensitive data leaks in Android apps. However, traditional static tools, such as FlowDroid, still face well-known challenges due to the complexity of accurately modeling the Android framework. In this paper, we investigate whether off-the-shelf Large Language Models (LLMs) can effectively reason about taint flows in Android apps. Our preliminary approach relies on an agentic interaction strategy, enabling the LLM to iteratively explore code and reason about data flows.
  We conduct an initial evaluation on the DroidBench benchmark against FlowDroid, where our approach outperforms the baseline: Gemini-3 Flash achieves an F1-score of 0.96, compared to 0.55 for FlowDroid. In particular, we observe improvements in challenging categories such as inter-component communication (0.95 vs. 0.17), implicit flows (0.94 vs. 0.00), and reflection (1.00 vs. 0.50), where FlowDroid typically struggles. On a small set of real-world apps, the LLM-based approach also identifies additional potential data leaks not reported by FlowDroid. These preliminary findings suggest that LLM reasoning may effectively complement traditional static taint analysis, motivating future research on hybrid LLM-enhanced taint analysis pipelines.

</details>


### [109] [Observability and Fault Injection for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24271)
*Zahra Seyedghorban,Egor Klimov,Arie van Deursen,Annibale Panichella,Burcu Kulahcioglu Ozkan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了llmmas-otel，一个基于OpenTelemetry分布式追踪和故障注入的轻量级、框架无关工具，用于对LLM多智能体软件工程系统进行可检查、可调试和可评估的受控故障分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统在软件工程任务中日益普及，但这些系统难以检查、调试和在受控故障条件下进行评估。

Method: 该工具结合OpenTelemetry分布式追踪与故障注入，对智能体在工作流阶段、智能体步骤、智能体间通信、工具调用和LLM调用等层面进行追踪对齐的遥测数据采集，并支持在选定的交互点进行定向故障注入。

Result: 工具支持基线执行与故障执行的可重现对比，并能通过对齐的追踪数据和运行产物检查故障影响。已在最小演示工作流和真实LLM多智能体软件开发系统上完成初步验证。

Conclusion: llmmas-otel为LLM多智能体软件工程系统的可观测性、故障调试和受控评估提供了一个可行的解决方案，未来有望支持更广泛的系统验证。

Abstract: Large Language Model-based multi-agent systems are increasingly explored for software engineering tasks, but they remain difficult to inspect, debug, and evaluate under controlled failures. We present llmmas-otel, a lightweight and framework-agnostic tool that combines OpenTelemetry-based distributed tracing with fault injection for LLM-based multi-agent systems in software engineering workflows. The tool instruments agent executions with trace-aligned telemetry across workflow phases, agent steps, inter-agent communication, tool calls, and LLM invocations, and supports targeted fault injection at selected interaction points. This makes it possible to compare baseline and faulty executions in a reproducible way and inspect the effects through aligned traces and run artifacts. We describe the motivation, architecture, implementation, current capabilities, and initial validation of the tool on a minimal demo workflow and a real LLM-based multi-agent system for software development.

</details>


### [110] [Ockhamareto: Pareto-Gated Segment-Level Credit Assignment for Concise Unit-Test Generation with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24473)
*Dong Huang,Mark Harman,Jie M. Zhang,Zhijiang Guo,Mingzhe Du,See Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了Ockhamareto，一个基于奥卡姆剃刀原理和帕累托最优的单次GRPO框架，用于单元测试生成与选择。它通过帕累托门控奖励和Token级分段信用机制，在突变分数和测试数量两个目标上实现严格帕累托支配，显著超越现有最强RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单元测试生成需要在测试效果（突变分数）和测试效率（测试数量）之间做出权衡，现有方法往往无法同时优化这两个目标，缺乏一种系统性的方法来找到每个待测函数的最优权衡点。

Method: 包含两个核心组件：(i) 帕累托门控奖励，仅奖励在突变分数和测试数量负空间中非支配的rollout；(ii) Token级分段信用，将每个测试的边缘突变击杀归因回其单元测试块的token。

Result: 在UnLeakedTestBench上严格帕累托支配最强RL基线MIST-RL，突变分数提升49.9%对31.3%（N=5），平均测试数量更少（2.60 vs 4.67），实现3.4倍的每测试权衡改进。在全部四个基准测试和所有模型规模（4B/9B/27B）上均优于现有最先进方法，突变分数提升30-35个百分点。

Conclusion: 帕累托前沿的最优权衡膝点与函数大小等易计算的代理指标不相关，因此必须通过显式计算帕累托前沿来识别每个待测函数的关键工程权衡。

Abstract: We introduce \textbf{Ockhamareto}, a single-shot GRPO framework for unit-test generation and selection, based on the principles of \emph{Ockham's Razor} and \emph{Pareto Optimality}. Ockhamareto has two principal components: (i)~a \emph{Pareto-gated Bonus} that rewards only rollouts non-dominated in~(mutation, $-$\#tests) space, and (ii)~\emph{Token-level Segment Credit}, which attributes each test's marginal mutation kills back to the tokens of its unit-test block. On the \emph{UnLeakedTestBench~(ULT)}, Ockhamareto \emph{strictly Pareto-dominates} the strongest RL baseline~(\emph{MIST-RL}). Furthermore, it dominates on {\em each and all} optimization objectives, catching more bugs ($49.9\%$ vs $31.3\%$ mutation score at $N{=}5$), using \emph{fewer} tests ($2.60$ vs $4.67$ on average), thereby achieving $3.4\times$ the per-test trade-off improvement. The advantage is found in all four benchmarks~(\emph{HumanEval+}, \emph{MBPP+}, \emph{CodeContests}, \emph{TestGenEval-Lite}): Ockhamareto leads both mutation and coverage metrics on every one, always with the smallest suite. Ockhamareto also outperforms the state-of-the-art at all model scales, adding $+30$--$35$~pp mutation at 4B, 9B, and 27B model sizes. We also show that the knee point of the optimal trade-off between efficiency and effectiveness on the Pareto front is not correlated with obvious more easily computed proxy metrics, such as function size. This finding motivates the Pareto front computation; it is needed to identify this crucial engineering trade-off for each function under test.

</details>


### [111] [LumiXAI: A Modular Full-Stack Framework for Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24524)
*Alfio Ferrara,Lorenzo Gatta,Sergio Picascia,Elisabetta Rocchetti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LumiXAI，一个模块化的全栈可解释性框架，将分类和生成式归因分析整合到单一系统中，提供交互式GUI、插件架构和三个访问层级（非程序员、开发者、扩展者），并通过容器化服务和持久化结果确保分析的可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征归因工具分散且碎片化，各工具仅专注于单一维度（如单一模态、单一API或GUI、固定方法集），难以兼顾多方面能力。同时，许多可解释性工具面向领域专家设计，需要编程技能或方法知识，对非专家用户不友好。

Method: LumiXAI采用模块化全栈架构，将分类和生成式归因耦合，提供支持双向探索的交互式GUI，内置插件架构可注册新模型和方法，并通过三个访问层级（非程序员、开发者、扩展者）从统一后端服务不同用户群体。

Result: 该系统在统一接口、统一交互模型和统一持久层下运营多种成熟的归因方法，通过容器化服务实现跨机器的可复现分析。

Conclusion: LumiXAI作为统一的可解释性分析系统，解决了现有工具的碎片化问题，使归因分析的访问门槛降低并提高了可复现性。

Abstract: Feature attribution is a central tool of model interpretability, yet the software through which it is applied remains fragmented: individual tools specialize along narrow axes, such as a single modality, a code API or a GUI, or a fixed rather than extensible method set, and rarely combine these strengths. Moreover, many explainability tools are designed primarily for domain experts, requiring programming skills or familiarity with attribution methods that can make them difficult for non-expert users to access. In this article, we present LumiXAI, a modular full-stack framework that consolidates attribution analysis into a single system. It couples classification and generative attribution with an interactive GUI supporting bidirectional exploration, a plug-in architecture for registering new models and methods, and three access tiers serving non-programmers, developers, and extenders from one backend. Its contribution is a system that operationalises established attribution methods under one interface, one interaction model, and one persistence layer, with containerised services and persistent results making analyses reproducible across machines.

</details>


### [112] [Quantifying the Relationship Between Team Dysfunctions and Performance in Capstone Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24634)
*Luciano Pereira Soares,Luciana Campos Lima,Rafael Corsi Ferrão*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究基于Lencioni团队五功能障碍模型，利用学生团队调查数据探讨了工程Capstone项目中团队动态与项目绩效之间的关系，发现二者存在弱到中等程度的关联，并强调目标一致性和交付物关注的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Capstone项目被视为评估工程和计算机科学学生职业准备度的基础，团队协作是关键能力之一，但团队动态与项目绩效之间的关系尚未被充分理解。

Method: 通过学生团队调查数据，基于Lencioni的五功能障碍模型量化团队动态，所有测量项采用反向编码（得分范围3-9，值越高代表功能障碍越低），每个功能障碍得分由三个对应调查项求和得出。

Result: 团队动态与项目绩效之间存在弱到中等程度的相关关系。

Conclusion: 团队动态应作为技术因素和情境因素之外的补充维度加以考虑，目标对齐和交付物专注是提高Capstone项目绩效的关键，该发现为Capstone项目的设计提供了实证依据。

Abstract: This paper investigates the association between team dysfunctions and performance in the context of an engineering Capstone program. Capstone programs are considered fundamental in assessing the readiness of engineering and computer science students for professional practice, evaluating not only technical knowledge but also essential competencies such as teamwork, communication, design, and organization. Despite the recognized importance of team dynamics in such settings, their relationship with project performance remains insufficiently understood. Using survey data collected from student teams, we quantify team dynamics based on Lencioni's model of the five dysfunctions of a team, with all measures reverse-coded so that higher values indicate more positive team behaviors (i.e., lower dysfunction). The results indicate a weak-to-moderate relationship between team dynamics and performance. Each dysfunction score was computed as the sum of three corresponding survey items. All items were coded such that higher values indicate more positive team behaviors (i.e., lower dysfunction). Therefore, each score ranges from 3 to 9, where higher values reflect healthier team dynamics. This study analyzes empirical evidence on team effectiveness in project-based learning environments and offers insights for the design of Capstone programs. In particular, it highlights the importance of goal alignment and focus on deliverables while emphasizing that team dynamics should be considered alongside technical and contextual factors.

</details>


### [113] [Aging of Prompt Engineering Techniques Across LLM Versions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24641)
*Anastasiia Rudyk,Julian Oertel,Regina Hebig*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究对Khojah等人(2025)的研究进行了部分复现，评估五种提示工程技术在三个LLM版本对（GPT-3.5-Turbo/GPT-4o、Qwen2/Qwen2.5、Mistral-7B/Mistral-Large）上的函数级代码生成效果。结果表明提示工程的"时效性"因模型家族而异：GPT新模型对结构化提示增益递减甚至为负，Qwen仍受益于Few-Shot和CCoT，Mistral表现混合。因此提示策略须按模型家族和代际适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新LLM频繁发布，但提示工程技术在不同代际LLM之间的有效性变化尚不明确，需要系统评估其跨代际的稳定性与迁移性。

Method: 对Khojah等(2025)进行部分复现，在六个指令微调模型（三个版本对）上评估五种提示技术（Zero-Shot、Few-Shot、CoT、CCoT、改编版PoT），使用CodePromptEval数据集中的218个上下文丰富Python函数，通过pass@k功能正确性指标评估19,620个生成结果。

Result: 提示工程以模型家族特有的方式"老化"：GPT新模型结构化提示边际增益递减甚至为负；Qwen模型继续显著受益于Few-Shot和CCoT；Mistral模型表现混合，CCoT持续有效但CoT和PoT收益减弱。

Conclusion: 有效的提示策略必须按模型家族和代际进行适配，而非不加修改地迁移。这为未来自适应的、模型感知的提示方法以及更广泛的多维代码质量评估研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Prompt engineering and prompt engineering techniques (PETs) have become an integral part of software engineering for AI systems. However, new LLMs are released frequently and it remains unclear how the effectiveness of prompt engineering techniques changes across successive generations of Large Language Models (LLMs). To this end, we conduct a partial replication of the study by Khojah et al. (2025). We evaluate five techniques - Zero-Shot, Few-Shot, Chain-of-Thought (CoT), Contrastive Chain-of-Thought (CCoT), and an adapted version of Program-of-Thought (PoT) - on six instruction-tuned models grouped into three version pairs: GPT-3.5-Turbo/GPT-4o, Qwen2 7B Instruct/Qwen2.5 7B Instruct, and Mistral-7B-Instruct/Mistral-Large. We use a cleaned subset of the CodePromptEval dataset with 218 context-rich Python functions and 19,620 total generations assessed via pass@k-based functional correctness to evaluate model pairs on function-level code generation tasks. We show that prompt engineering "ages" in a model-family-specific way: Newer GPT models exhibit diminishing or even negative marginal gains from structured prompting, suggesting that instruction-following and reasoning scaffolds are increasingly internalized, whereas Qwen models continue to benefit substantially from Few-Shot and CCoT. Mistral models show mixed behavior with persistent gains from CCoT but attenuated benefits from CoT and PoT. Our results imply that effective prompting strategies must be adapted per model family and generation rather than transferred unchanged. This motivates future work on adaptive, model-aware prompting and broader, multi-dimensional code quality evaluation.

</details>


### [114] [A Literate Programming Environment for Human and Machine Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24644)
*Adam T. Burke*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于构建文学化编程（literate programming）环境的系统，该系统通过与语言感知的机器学习代理协同工作，将代码、自然语言和结构化数据共置一处，更高效地利用LLM上下文窗口，并为LLM编程代理提供类似人类IDE的符号感知搜索工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编程代理缺乏类似人类IDE中符号感知搜索和使用信息的能力，同时LLM上下文窗口有限，需要更高效地利用上下文空间。将代码与其相关自然语言和结构化数据共置可以提高LLM的理解和生成质量。

Method: 构建了一个包含四部分的环境：可执行程序散文的语法（grammar）、将名称作为一等对象的解析器、关联散文/名称/可执行工件（artifacts）的内部名称图（name-graph）、以及用于现有语言和测试工具集的绑定机制。实现支持三种既定编程语言，并提供了多个示例程序。

Result: 实现了可工作的系统，成功绑定了三种成熟的编程语言，并提供了若干示例程序来展示该环境的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将代码与其自然语言和结构化数据上下文共置并引入名称图机制，该环境能够更高效地利用LLM上下文窗口，并为LLM编码代理提供更接近人类程序员IDE体验的符号感知工具集，从而改善文学化编程与人机协作的实践。

Abstract: This paper introduces an environment for constructing literate programs in concert with language-aware machine agents. This environment includes a grammar for executable program essays, a parser that treats names as first-class objects, an internal name-graph which relates prose, names and executable artifacts, and a binding mechanism for existing languages and testing toolsets. This supports co-location of code with its most relevant natural language and structured data context, making better use of Large Language Model (LLM) context windows. It also provides LLM coding agents with a toolset more analogous to the symbol-aware search and usage information available in human programmer-facing Integrated Development Environments (IDEs). We describe a working implementation with bindings to three established programming languages, and several example programs.

</details>


### [115] [Causal Explanations of Process Monitor Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24672)
*Tom Yaacov,Nathan Blake,Hana Chockler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于实际因果框架的流程监控预测局部解释方法，通过构建面向流程的因果模型来捕捉事件间的时间依赖关系，从而计算事件对预测结果的影响和责任。该方法在真实事件日志数据集上比现有局部解释方法产生更稳定、更简洁的解释，同时保持高效的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测流程监控（PPM）中的AI模型虽然预测性能高，但黑盒性质使其难以理解预测背后的原因，现有解释方法未能充分利用流程的结构特征和事件间的时间依赖关系。

Method: 提出一种基于实际因果性框架的局部（案例级）解释方法：定义一个面向流程的因果模型来捕捉轨迹中事件之间的时间依赖关系，该方法隐式利用该模型计算事件原因并量化事件对预测结果的重要性，并提出一个实用的、模型无关的算法来近似事件责任。

Result: 在多达130,000条轨迹、最长1,800个事件、最多400种事件类型的真实事件日志数据集上进行了评估，与最先进的局部解释方法相比，该方法产生了更稳定和更简洁的解释，同时保持竞争性的效率。

Conclusion: 基于实际因果框架的流程感知解释方法在稳定性和简洁性上优于现有方法，为预测流程监控中的黑盒AI模型提供了更有意义的局部解释。

Abstract: Process mining is widely used to diagnose processes and identify performance and compliance issues. Specifically, Predictive Process Monitoring (PPM) techniques use AI models to predict outcomes of ongoing process instances. While these models can achieve high predictive performance, their black-box nature makes it difficult to understand the underlying reasons behind their output predictions. In this paper, we propose a novel approach for generating local (case-level) explanations of process monitor predictions based on the framework of actual causality. We define a causal model tailored to processes that captures temporal dependencies between events in a trace, thus allowing us to reason about causal influence of events on the predicted outcome. Our method uses this model implicitly to compute causes and quantify the importance of different events with respect to the predicted outcome. We present a practical, model-agnostic algorithm that approximates event responsibility given the process structure reflected in the causal model. We evaluate our approach on a range of datasets derived from real-life event logs from a standard PPM benchmark. Each dataset contains up to 130,000 traces, with trace lengths of up to 1,800 events and up to 400 distinct event types. We compare our approach with state-of-the-art local explanation methods. The results demonstrate that our approach produces more stable and concise explanations while maintaining competitive efficiency.

</details>


### [116] [''You Can't Open an LLM With a Screwdriver'': The De-Democratization of Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24720)
*Zixuan Feng,Italo Santos,Kostadin Damevski,Anita Sarma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇观点论文，反驳"生成式AI将终结编程"的说法，指出AI虽能扩大代码生成的"可及性"，但软件工程的专业技能并不会消失，而是从编码本身转向意图规范、评估与系统集成等更高层次的控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对"生成式AI即将取代所有编程工作"的普遍预测，作者认为这种观点忽略了可及性（access）与控制力（control）之间的本质区别，需要重新审视AI时代软件工程专业知识的真正价值所在。

Method: 基于专家小组讨论，通过概念辨析（区分"可及性"与"控制"），论证AI并非消除软件工程专长，而是将其重心转移至意图规范、AI行为治理、软件行为评估和系统集成等关键环节。

Result: 论文指出AI扩大了代码生成的可及性，但控制权可能更加集中于少数掌握软件实践、基础设施、评估方法和部署流程的人手中；软件工程专业知识的重心正转向意图规范方向。

Conclusion: 研究为软件工程社区在AI时代提出了教育、工具和政策三方面的研究机会，以帮助从业者获得更大的自主性、问责性和适应性。

Abstract: Claims that generative AI will soon write all of the code have led to predictions that programming is nearing its end. In this vision paper, we argue against this assumption that broader access to code generation necessarily democratizes software development, i.e., everyone can code but we have to distinguish between access and control: by access, we mean the ability of more people, including non-experts and less-experienced developers, to generate code-like artifacts with AI; by control, we mean the capacity to inspect, evaluate, integrate, maintain, and govern those artifacts as dependable software. While AI may broaden access to code production, control may become more concentrated among those who own or understand the code, software practices, infrastructure, evaluation practices, and deployment pipelines.
  Grounded in an expert panel, our vision paper argues that AI does not eliminate software engineering expertise but shifts where that expertise becomes most critical. The locus of software engineering expertise is shifting toward intent specification: orchestrating and governing AI behavior, evaluating software behavior, and integrating software systems. We conclude this paper by identifying research opportunities for education, tools, and policy that can help the software engineering community respond to the AI era with greater agency, accountability, and adaptability.

</details>


### [117] [From Natural Language Requirements to Graphical User Interfaces: Automated Prototyping and Verification with Pretrained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24749)
*Kristian Kolthoff*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对两个挑战：将自然语言需求转化为GUI原型的成本高昂（C1），以及GUI应用中原型需求验证仍以人工为主（C2）。作者提出基于NL的GUI检索与重排方法、LLM适配GUI生成的技巧，以及基于LLM的静态原型验证方法和多模态LLM代理用于动态GUI应用的需求验证，显著降低了两方面的人工工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求获取对交互式软件系统开发至关重要，但自然语言存在固有歧义；形式化规范虽能减少歧义却需要专业知识；GUI原型虽是有效替代方案，但高保真原型的创建耗时费力。同时，需求验证仍主要依赖人工，现有自动化方法多局限于静态规则技术。

Method: 针对C1，提出基于自然语言的GUI检索与重排序方法、新基准以及高效适配LLM进行GUI生成的技术（包括专有GUI表示）。针对C2，提出基于LLM的方法在静态GUI原型上验证语义复杂的自然语言需求，并引入多模态LLM代理，通过自动生成和评估的交互轨迹在动态GUI应用中验证复杂功能性和非功能性需求。

Result: 所提方法在大规模带人工标注的基准上得到验证，有效证明了其性能；在GUI原型生成和需求验证两方面均显著减少了人工工作量。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地解决了从自然语言到GUI原型自动生成以及需求自动化验证两大核心难题，提出的LLM驱动方法在减少人工投入方面取得了实质性进展。

Abstract: Requirements elicitation is essential for developing interactive software systems, as it helps ensure that the resulting product meets stakeholder needs. Since elicitation typically relies on natural language (NL), misunderstandings can arise from its inherent ambiguity. Formal specifications can reduce ambiguity but require technical expertise. GUI prototyping therefore provides a valuable alternative by turning requirements into tangible visual artifacts that support communication, elicitation, and validation. However, creating high-fidelity prototypes remains time-consuming and costly. Similarly, requirements verification, which ensures that implementations conform to specified requirements, is still largely manual, while existing automated approaches are often limited to static, rule-based techniques. This work addresses two challenges: (C1) reducing the effort required to transform NL requirements into GUI prototypes, and (C2) reducing the effort required for requirements verification in GUI applications and prototypes. For C1, we introduce novel NL-based GUI retrieval and reranking methods, new benchmarks, and techniques for efficiently adapting LLMs to GUI generation, including proprietary GUI representations. Their effectiveness is demonstrated on a large benchmark with human annotations. For C2, we propose LLM-based methods for verifying semantically complex NL requirements on static GUI prototypes and introduce a multimodal LLM-based agent for verifying complex functional and non-functional requirements in dynamic GUI applications through automatically generated and evaluated interaction trajectories. Overall, the proposed methods substantially reduce manual effort in GUI prototyping and requirements verification.

</details>


### [118] [Automatic Model Card Generation Using an LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24807)
*Tajkia Rahman Toma,Balreet Grewal,Cor-Paul Bezemer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出两个基于LLM的模型卡处理系统：MCTidy（将现有模型卡重组为标准模板）和MCGenie（从模型仓库数据直接生成模型卡）。在48个Hugging Face模型卡上的评估显示，MCTidy具有高信息保留率和稳定性，MCGenie生成的模型卡语义相似度高（约0.9），过半完全正确，生成质量高度依赖资源可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型卡存在结构不一致、许多模型缺少模型卡等问题，导致模型比较和解释困难，需要一个标准化的解决方案来提升模型文档的一致性、可比较性和覆盖率。

Method: 提出两个系统：MCTidy利用LLM将现有模型卡重组为标准化模板；MCGenie利用LLM直接从模型仓库数据生成模型卡。评估维度包括信息保留、章节对齐、幻觉和稳定性（MCTidy），以及语义相似性、事实正确性和资源敏感性（MCGenie）。

Result: MCTidy在48个Hugging Face模型卡上表现出高信息保留率、准确的章节分配、罕见的幻觉（主要在描述性部分）和强稳定性。MCGenie生成的模型卡语义相似度均值约0.9，超过一半完全正确，大多数剩余错误为轻微错误，且生成质量与支持资源（尤其是论文）的可用性密切相关。

Conclusion: 研究验证了基于LLM的方法能够实现可扩展、标准化的模型卡文档生成，为推进机器学习模型的透明度、可用性和问责制提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Model cards are structured documents that summarize key information about machine learning models to improve transparency, usability, and accountability. However, they often lack a consistent structure, and many models provide no model cards, making comparison and interpretation difficult. This paper presents two contributions. First, we propose MCTidy, an LLM-based approach that reorganizes existing model cards into a standardized template to improve clarity and comparability. Second, we introduce MCGenie, an LLM-based system that generates model cards directly from model repository data. We apply MCTidy to 48 Hugging Face model cards and evaluate information retention, section alignment, hallucination, and stability. Our findings show high information retention with minimal textual loss, accurate section assignment, rare hallucinations primarily in descriptive sections, and strong stability across runs. We assess MCGenie by generating model cards for the same 48 models and assessing semantic similarity, factual correctness, and sensitivity to input resources. The generated model cards achieved high semantic similarity (mean around 0.9); over half were fully correct, and most remaining errors were minor. Generation quality depended strongly on the availability of supporting resources, particularly associated papers. Overall, our findings demonstrate the potential of LLM-based methods to enable scalable, standardized model card documentation.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [A Survey of Timing Variability in Microservice-Based Software-Defined Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23649)
*Cyrus K. Vattes,Habib Mostafaei,Nirvana Meratnia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本篇综述论文探讨了微服务系统时序变异性的来源、传播机制和可观测影响，并以软件定义汽车为实例，比较了各类时序模型在负载稳定性、执行结构、资源共享、可观测性和保证可接受性等方面的假设，总结了它们在微服务架构下的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统具有模块化和适应性的优点，但其分布式结构使得时序行为难以分析和保证，局部时序扰动可能传播为系统级问题，因此需要对时序变异性的来源、传播机制及现有建模方法的局限性进行系统性梳理。

Method: 采用综述研究方法，以软件定义汽车作为动机示例，对时序变异性的来源、传播机制和可观测影响进行分析；系统比较了多类时序模型，评估其对负载稳定性、执行结构、资源共享、可观测性和保证可接受性等方面的假设，并总结其在微服务场景下的局限性。

Result: 识别了微服务系统中时序变异性的关键来源和传播路径，并对主流时序建模方法进行了分类比较，明确了各模型在微服务架构（特别是软件定义汽车场景）中的适用性和不足。

Conclusion: 现有时序模型难以全面覆盖微服务系统在分布式资源竞争、中间件交互和动态负载等因素下产生的时序不确定性，需要进一步开发适用于微服务架构的时序分析方法与保证机制。

Abstract: Microservice-based systems are modular and adaptable, but their distributed structure makes their timing behavior difficult to analyze and guarantee. Because latency emerges from interactions among service dependencies, shared resources, and coordinating middleware, local timing disturbances can propagate into system-level effects. This survey examines the sources, propagation mechanisms, and observable impacts of timing variability in microservice-based systems, using software-defined vehicles as a motivating example. It compares major classes of timing models by their assumptions about workload stability, execution structure, resource sharing, observability, and guarantee admissibility, and summarizes their limitations within microservice-based software-defined vehicles.

</details>


### [120] [Memory-Sovereign Inference: Output-Exact Execution Beyond Full Residency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23805)
*Lukas Stepanek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种可证伪的证书框架，用于区分存储后备推理中的表示、语义需求、调度请求和流量等概念，并通过Qwen3-Next模型的严格实验验证了异步推理路径的正确性和资源边界合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 存储后备推理的声明容易被过度夸大：进程RSS排除了计费页面缓存、进程本地设备读数排除了板级使用、成功生成并不能确立正确的异步重用。现有方法缺乏可证伪的验证机制来区分这些概念。

Method: 提出可证伪证书框架，区分表示、语义需求、调度请求与流量，同时命名资源权威、精确性时间范围和测试的重用转换。通过LRU64执行、零缓存oracle对比、匹配源码campaign以及六个交叉平衡对等实验进行严格验证。

Result: 48个层专家对象的43.59375 GiB语义需求下界超过声明的34 GiB完整驻留包络。异步组件与阻塞直连路径相比，墙钟时间仅为其32.3%，且物理源字节相同；64个token ID、全部logit行、3408个路由事件完全精确；33,481个memory.max事件在缓冲路径中被记录。

Conclusion: 异步组件在相同输出字节下显著提升性能，且成功通过所有预设的精确性检验和故障单元测试；证书仅在命名转换下支持fail-closed行为，主实验范围限定为固定模型、工作负载、运行时、设备及64输出时间范围。

Abstract: Storage-backed inference is easy to overclaim: process RSS excludes charged page cache, process-local device readings exclude board-wide use, and successful generation does not establish correct asynchronous reuse. We present a falsifiable certificate separating representation, semantic demand, scheduler requests, and traffic while naming resource authorities, exactness horizons, and tested reuse transitions.
  At one Qwen3-Next identity, the stock router selects all 48 x 512 managed layer-expert objects during 32K prefill. Their duplicate-free, overlap-free canonical union gives a 43.59375 GiB semantic-demand lower bound, exceeding the declared 34 GiB full-residency envelope and the 11 GiB host-hard plus physical 24 GiB-device envelope. LRU64 execution stays within its host-hard/GPU-audited contract. Against one prespecified zero-cache oracle, all 64 token IDs, every byte of 64 complete 151,936-float logit rows, 3,408 route events, response bytes, and recorded consumer and destination identities are exact. Recurrent-state and upstream-runtime equality are excluded.
  In a matched source campaign, the buffered path completes exactly but reaches the 11 GiB host ceiling and records 33,481 memory.max events. The blocking one-window direct path and complete eight-window asynchronous component are exact with positive margin and zero limit events. Across six counterbalanced pairs, the complete asynchronous component takes 32.3% of the blocking direct path's wall time at identical physical source bytes per output; the comparison jointly changes queue depth, overlap, and lifecycle implementation. Separately, fourteen prespecified control/fault cells pass across later Qwen3-Next and Gemma 4 binaries, supporting fail-closed behavior only for the named transitions. The principal experiment is one fixed model, workload, runtime, device, and 64-output horizon.

</details>


### [121] [ShardMeter: Sharded and Geo-Distributed Training Without the Guesswork](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23840)
*Tim Beringer,Patrick Diem,Felix Wolf,Arya Mazaheri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ShardMeter是一种轻量级分析性能模型，可预测跨任意分片、分布式甚至去中心化训练场景中基于Transformer工作负载的端到端运行时间。它根据模型特征和目标硬件拓扑，估算每GPU和每岛吞吐量、训练成本、总墙钟时间，并识别性能瓶颈，帮助用户快速探索配置空间并选择接近最优的部署方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模AI模型训练常超出单一数据中心的能力，需要分片、多集群和去中心化训练。然而资源分配空间巨大，穷举基准测试和手动调优不可行，且性能受模型大小、GPU内存、批大小、带宽和分片策略等多个紧密耦合因素的影响。

Method: ShardMeter采用轻量级分析方法构建性能模型，输入为模型特性和目标硬件拓扑，输出包括每GPU和每岛吞吐量、训练成本、总墙钟时间以及性能瓶颈识别。

Result: 分析揭示了岛屿规模增大时的收益递减规律，量化了计算受限与通信受限扩展之间的转换，评估了超参数权衡，并为大规模去中心化训练建模了成本-吞吐量关系。

Conclusion: ShardMeter能够快速探索配置空间，选择接近最优的部署计划，避免昂贵且耗时的试错过程。

Abstract: Training large-scale AI models often outgrows a single data center, demanding sharded, multi-cluster, and decentralized training. However, the huge space of resource allocations makes exhaustive benchmarking and manual tuning impractical, while performance depends on tightly coupled factors like model size, GPU memory, batch size, bandwidth, and sharding strategy. We introduce ShardMeter, a lightweight analytical performance model that predicts the end-to-end runtime of transformer-based workloads across arbitrary sharded, distributed, and even decentralized training. Given a model's characteristics and a target hardware topology, ShardMeter estimates per-GPU and per-island throughput, training cost, total wall-clock time, and identifies performance bottlenecks. Our analysis reveals diminishing-return regimes as island size increases, quantifies transitions between compute- and communication-bound scaling, evaluates hyperparameter trade-offs, and models cost-throughput for large-scale decentralized training. ShardMeter exposes these insights to quickly explore the configuration space, choose near-optimal deployment plans, and avoid costly trial and error.

</details>


### [122] [A Few Shared Random Bits Suffice for Constant-Round Almost Stable Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24102)
*Yijun Chang,Kushagra Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在常数轮内求解几乎稳定匹配的分布式算法。在congest模型中，仅使用少量共享随机比特，即可在O(log(1/ε)/ε⁴)轮内计算出期望阻塞对数量不超过ε|E|的匹配。核心创新是"度保护冻结规则"，使得广泛变化的度数可以通过单一的全局论证处理，避免了以往算法中Θ(log n)个连续度数阈值的开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往算法仅在有限度或几乎正则图上实现常数轮复杂度；在一般图上，轮复杂度依赖于n的polylogarithmic倍数。本文旨在消除这种对顶点数和最大度的依赖，实现一般二分图上的常数轮几乎稳定匹配。

Method: 提出"度保护冻结规则"（degree-guarded freezing rule），通过单一的全局charging论证处理广泛变化的度数，避免了此前使用的Θ(log n)个连续度数阈值。共享随机比特仅用于选择一个共同随机输出迭代。分别给出有预共享随机性、无预共享随机性（通过低直径分解）以及MPC模型下的算法变体。

Result: 在congest模型中，使用O(log(1/ε))个共享随机比特，在O(log(1/ε)/ε⁴)轮内得到期望阻塞对≤ε|E|的匹配；对每个常数ε>0，轮复杂度为O(1)。无预共享随机性时复杂度为O(log(1/ε)/ε⁴ + log n/ε)轮；MPC模型中为O(log(1/ε)/ε⁴)轮（线性总内存）。

Conclusion: 本文首次在一般二分图上实现了常数轮的几乎稳定匹配算法，消除了对图规模和最大度的依赖，为分布式稳定匹配问题提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We show that almost stable matching can be solved in constant distributed rounds on general bipartite graphs $G=(V,E)$ using only a few shared random bits. Specifically, in the $\congest$ model, we compute a matching whose expected number of blocking pairs is at most $\varepsilon |E|$ in $O\left(\frac{\log(1/\varepsilon)}{\varepsilon^4}\right)$ rounds using $O\left(\log(1/\varepsilon)\right)$ shared random bits. Thus, for every constant $\varepsilon>0$, the round complexity is $O(1)$, independent of the number of vertices and the maximum degree.
  Previous algorithms achieve constant round complexity only for bounded-degree or almost-regular graphs; on general graphs, their round complexity depends polylogarithmically on $n$. Our main technical idea is a degree-guarded freezing rule that allows widely varying degrees to be handled by a single global charging argument, avoiding the $Θ(\log n)$ successive degree thresholds used in previous work. The shared random bits are used only to select a common random output iteration.
  As consequences, we obtain an $O\left(
  \frac{\log(1/\varepsilon)}{\varepsilon^4}
  +
  \frac{\log n}{\varepsilon} \right)$-round $\congest$ algorithm without pre-shared randomness, via a low-diameter decomposition, and an $O\left(\frac{\log(1/\varepsilon)}{\varepsilon^4}\right)$-round algorithm in the fully-scalable Massively Parallel Computation ($\mpc$) model with linear total memory.

</details>


### [123] [pigzpp: Fast, Parallel, Portable Compression for the Whole Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24153)
*Thamme Gowda*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: pigzpp是一个从零重写的C++23线程安全压缩库，将pigz的过程全局可变状态改造为可重入的加速DEFLATE核心，并暴露给C++、Python、WebAssembly、Go和Rust等语言。它提供多条目ZIP归档和快速PNG编解码器，支持zlib-ng和Intel ISA-L两种后端。在文本语料上，zlib-ng后端达到pigz CLI吞吐量的2.1倍，ISA-L后端达到8.5倍；相比Python内存gzip则分别提升16倍和50倍。输出保持标准兼容，可跨gzip、pigz和unzip解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: pigz虽然广泛部署，但其过程全局可变状态设计使其无法作为可重入、可直接嵌入的库使用。作者希望将pigz的能力转化为一个线程安全、跨语言、可嵌入的压缩库，同时保持输出标准兼容性。

Method: 从零开始用C++23重写pigz，将设计重构为线程安全库，以单一加速DEFLATE核心为基础，通过语言绑定暴露给C++、Python、WebAssembly、Go和Rust。在应用层面构建多条目ZIP归档和PNG编解码器。提供zlib-ng（便携、保比率）和Intel ISA-L（x86-64快速路径）两个后端，前者保持gzip/zlib压缩比，后者以约10%更大输出换取更高速度。

Result: zlib-ng后端达到pigz CLI吞吐量的2.1倍和Python内存gzip的16倍；ISA-L后端分别达到8.5倍和50倍。ISA-L在级别6下输出比gzip/zlib大约10%。所有输出均为标准兼容，可被gzip、pigz和unzip交叉解码。

Conclusion: pigzpp提供了一个自包含、多平台的压缩技术栈，展示了在自动化兼容性测试下进行AI辅助现代化改造的可行性案例。

Abstract: pigz is a widely deployed parallel gzip utility, but its process-global mutable state means that it was not designed as a reentrant, directly embeddable library. pigzpp is a from-scratch C++23 rewrite that turns the design into a thread-safe library with one accelerated DEFLATE core exposed to C++, Python, WebAssembly, Go, and Rust. As application-level conveniences built on that same core, it also provides multi-entry ZIP archives and a fast PNG codec. Its portable zlib-ng backend preserves gzip/zlib's ratio; its Intel ISA-L backend is an x86-64-only fast path that produces about 10% larger output at level 6 on our text corpus. In that configuration, zlib-ng reaches 2.1 times the CLI throughput of pigz and 16 times that of Python's in-memory gzip; ISA-L reaches 8.5 times and 50 times, respectively. Outputs remain standards-compliant and cross-decode with gzip, pigz, and unzip. The result is a self-contained, multi-platform compression stack and a case study in AI-assisted modernization under automated compatibility tests.

</details>


### [124] [An HPC Approach to Accelerate Tensor Decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24307)
*Markus Hellgren,Erna Begovic Kovac,Hans O. Karlsson,Roman Iakymchuk*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种新的Jacobi型张量分解算法，开发了基于CUDA的GPU实现，支持任意阶张量。在NVIDIA H100上测试显示，该算法对高达九维的可对角化张量能正确收敛，运行时间可预测地随张量阶数增长，且相比原始MATLAB参考实现性能提升超过两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统复杂度增长极快，即使简单的模型也难以模拟，迫切需要能够处理高维数据（张量）的方法。

Method: 提出一种新型Jacobi型张量分解算法，并开发基于CUDA的GPU实现，支持任意阶张量，在单GPU上运行。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100 GPU上测试，算法对高达九维的可对角化张量正确收敛，运行时间随张量阶数增长呈可预测方式扩展，且性能比原始MATLAB参考实现快两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 所提出的CUDA张量分解算法在正确性和性能上均表现优异，显著优于MATLAB参考实现，为高维张量处理提供了高效的GPU解决方案。

Abstract: Quantum systems grow in complexity so rapidly that even modest models become difficult to simulate, creating a strong need for methods that can handle high-dimensional data, also known as tensors. In this work, we investigate a novel Jacobi-type tensor algorithm for tensor decomposition and develop a CUDA-based algorithm that supports tensors of arbitrary order on a single GPU. We test the implementation on NVIDIA H100 GPUs and show that the algorithm converges correctly for diagonalizable tensors up to nine dimensions, with runtime scaling in a predictable way as tensor order grows. Finally, our general algorithm outperforms the original MATLAB reference by more than two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [125] [Mixed-Precision SEM-Based CFD Simulations on GPUs: A Taylor-Green Vortex case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24348)
*Yanxiang Chen,Manuel Münsch,Roman Iakymchuk*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于谱元法（SEM）的CFD求解器Neko中混合精度策略的应用，提出三层层次化混合精度控制模型，在fp32边界组中实现了约34%的时间和能耗节省，同时提升了相对全局fp32的鲁棒性，表明混合精度应作为仿真级控制问题而非仅作为Krylov求解器优化来处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合精度是降低CFD模拟计算成本和能耗的有效方法，但其效果依赖于精度在仿真管线中的具体降低位置。已有研究往往将重点放在Krylov求解器的精度优化上，但作者通过性能分析发现流体时间步的运行时开销并非仅由Krylov收敛主导，速度与压力求解器仅需少量迭代，大量时间花费在SEM算子及其他求解器组件上，因此需要从仿真级视角重新审视混合精度策略。

Method: 采用Neko（一个基于谱元法的无矩阵CFD求解器）对Taylor-Green涡算例进行性能分析，提出三层层次化混合精度控制模型；在fp64和fp32两种精度边界约束下评估两组配置，并进一步探索针对特定操作的fp16内核覆盖。

Result: fp64边界组识别出精度敏感组件，表明SEM聚焦的fp32计算是未来优化的有前景方向；fp32边界组提供了主要实际收益，在高雷诺数算例中选定的配置相比fp64基线实现了约34%的时间和能耗降低，同时相比全局fp32提升了鲁棒性。fp16内核覆盖在特定操作上展现潜力，但梯度相关量（如涡量拟能）的敏感性增加。

Conclusion: 面向无矩阵SEM的CFD混合精度应当被视为仿真级控制问题，而非仅仅局限于Krylov求解器的优化；分层混合精度策略能够在保证鲁棒性的同时显著降低计算开销和能耗。

Abstract: Mixed precision is a promising approach for reducing the computational cost and energy consumption of Computation Fluid Dynamics (CFD) simulations, but its effectiveness depends strongly on where precision is reduced within the full simulation pipeline. In this work, we study Taylor-Green vortex case using Neko, a matrix-free CFD solver based on the spectral element method (SEM). Profiling shows that the fluid time step is not dominated by Krylov convergence alone: the velocity and pressure solvers require only a small number of iterations per step, while a substantial fraction of runtime is spent in other SEM operators and solver components. Motivated by this structure, we propose a three-level hierarchical mixed-precision control model. Two groups of configurations are evaluated in environments bounded by 64-bit floating-point (fp64) and 32-bit floating-point (fp32) precision, respectively. The fp64-bounded group identifies accuracy sensitive components and shows that SEM-focused fp32 computation is a promising direction for future optimization. The fp32-bounded group provides the main practical benefit. For the high Reynolds number case studied, selected configurations reduce both time- and energy-to-solution by about 34% relative to the fp64 baseline, while improving robustness compared with global fp32. Targeted fp16 kernel overrides are also explored, showing potential for selected operations but increased sensitivity in gradient-based quantities such as enstrophy. Overall, these results indicate that mixed-precision for matrix-free SEM-based CFD should be treated as a simulation-level control problem rather than solely as a Krylov-solver optimization.

</details>


### [126] [SatDL: Jointly Optimizing Data Redistribution and Training for Satellite-Based Distributed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24516)
*Hao Wu,Kin Whye Chew,Yizhan Han,Han Li,Jingxian Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SatDL，一个面向卫星分布式学习的智能数据再分配框架，通过Distributor-Critic机制联合建模并优化数据传输延迟与训练时间，以减少总端到端学习时间和星载能耗，同时保持推理精度接近最先进基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星分布式学习中，非IID数据（尤其是标签不平衡）严重拖慢训练收敛，延长训练时长并增加太阳能卫星的能耗。现有方法要么完全重新分配数据以强制IID（收敛快但通信延迟大），要么本地修改算法缓解标签不平衡（仍延长训练并增加能耗），两种极端都导致总端到端学习时间过长和能耗过高。

Method: SatDL提出一个数据再分配框架，核心是Distributor-Critic机制，对数据传输延迟和训练时间进行联合建模与联合优化，以最小化总端到端学习时间。

Result: 通过基于1,584颗卫星的Starlink星座轨迹驱动仿真和NVIDIA Jetson及A100 GPU硬件模拟（覆盖五个数据集）验证，SatDL将总端到端学习时间降低最高18.6%，星载能耗降低12.23%-88.00%，推理精度仅比最先进基线低几个百分点。

Conclusion: SatDL通过智能数据再分配，在数据传输延迟与训练时间之间取得最优平衡，显著减少卫星分布式学习的总端到端时间和能耗，为太阳能供电卫星的大规模在轨学习提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Satellite-based distributed learning promises to train machine-learning models directly in orbit using massive, globally dispersed sensor data, thereby avoiding large-scale data downloads to ground servers. However, training convergence is significantly slowed by severe non-IID data, specifically label imbalance, as each satellite observes different geographic regions with distinct labels. This imbalance extends training duration and increases energy consumption for solar-powered satellites. Existing approaches either fully redistribute data to enforce IID conditions - accelerating convergence but incurring substantial communication delays - or avoid redistribution entirely by modifying local learning algorithms to mitigate the impact of label imbalance, which, however, still prolong training and increase energy use. Both extremes result in excessive total end-to-end learning time (data-transfer delay plus training time) and thus elevated onboard energy consumption.
  We present SatDL, a data-redistribution framework designed to minimize total end-to-end learning time. At its core, SatDL develops a Distributor-Critic framework that jointly models and optimizes data-transfer delay and training time. Evaluations through trace-driven simulations of a 1,584-satellite Starlink constellation and hardware emulations using NVIDIA Jetson and A100 GPUs across five datasets show SatDL reduces total end-to-end learning time by up to 18.6% and onboard energy consumption by 12.23-88.00%, while maintaining inference accuracy within a few percentage points of state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [127] [Scalable datacenter replication with mostly-synchronous consensus on hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24622)
*Davide Rovelli,Philipp Berdesinski,Rodrigo Otoni,Patrick Eugster*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出scarHW，一种在FPGA智能网卡上实现的新型网络卡设计，通过创新的POPUC共识算法实现可扩展的一致性复制，大幅提升分布式服务的吞吐量和延迟，并在副本增加时仍保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式进程间的一致性数据复制（涉及共识问题）成本高昂且难以扩展，尤其在数据中心服务中性能要求严苛。现有系统要么规模小，要么放宽一致性保证，亟需一种可扩展且保持强一致性的方案。

Method: 设计scarHW网络卡，核心是POPUC共识算法，在FPGA智能网卡上实现。POPUC利用可编程网络设备的"近似同步"特性，实现"协作共识"（generalized consensus），允许多个并发决策，提升可扩展性同时不牺牲可用性。算法在TLA+中形式化验证，保证进程崩溃停止和消息发送/接收遗漏故障下的安全性。

Result: FPGA原型相比现有技术，将Redis和Zookeeper服务的吞吐量和延迟提升最多两个数量级。在少数副本故障时实现零停机，提供高鲁棒性、线速、可扩展的复制系统。

Conclusion: scarHW证明基于硬件的共识加速是解决分布式复制扩展性瓶颈的有效途径，为数据中心提供高性能、高可用的一致性复制基础设施。

Abstract: Consistent replication of data among distributed processes -- a task involving the well-known consensus problem -- is notoriously expensive and hard to scale, affecting especially datacenter services with stringent performance requirements. To mitigate this problem, we introduce scalable replication in-hardware ( scarHW ): a network card design that improves throughput and latency of consistent replication even when increasing the number of replicas, whereas current systems operate at a small scale or with relaxed consistency guarantees. At the heart of scarHW is our novel POPUC consensus algorithm, implemented in an FPGA smartNIC to take full advantage of the "mostly synchronous" behavior of programmable network devices in the datacenter. Unlike widely-adopted "mostly asynchronous" coordination protocols such as Paxos or leaderless alternatives, POPUC implements a generalized variant of consensus dubbed collaborative consensus which allows for several simultaneous decisions, achieving great scalability without compromising availability. POPUC preserves safety guarantees in the presence of process crash-stop and message send/receive omission failures (capturing incidental asynchrony) and has been formally specified and verified in TLA+. Our FPGA prototype improves throughput and latency of widely-used services Redis and Zookeeper by up to two orders of magnitude compared to the state of the art. scarHW-based services also achieve zero downtime upon failure of a minority of replicas, offering a highly-robust, wire-speed, scalable replication system.

</details>


### [128] [Asynchronous Verifiable Information Dispersal with Low Space and Communication Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24636)
*Thomas Locher,Yvonne-Anne Pignolet*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的异步可验证信息分散（AVID）协议，通过在二维矩阵中编码数据并设计专门的分散算法，同时实现了分散、存储、检索和恢复四个维度的低复杂度，在保持检索通信复杂度最优的同时显著改善了恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AVID协议在存储空间复杂度和数据检索通信复杂度上已达到最优，但数据分散和节点恢复这两个关键操作受到的关注不足，复杂度不够理想。

Method: 核心方法是将数据编码为二维矩阵，并设计专门的分散算法；此外还提出了一个变体方案，通过牺牲检索和恢复的通信复杂度来换取更低的存储空间复杂度和分散通信复杂度。

Result: 提出的协议在保持检索最优通信复杂度的同时，显著改善了恢复操作的复杂度；变体方案则在存储和分散复杂度上进一步优化，代价是检索和恢复复杂度升高。

Conclusion: 该协议在各项指标之间取得了良好平衡，适用于广泛的现实应用场景。

Abstract: The primary goal of a distributed storage system is to ensure that clients can both write and read data in a reliable and consistent manner, even in the presence of failures. While existing asynchronous verifiable information dispersal (AVID) protocols achieve optimal space complexity for storage and communication complexity for data retrieval in a Byzantine setting, the crucial operations of data dispersal and node recovery have received less attention.
  We propose an efficient AVID protocol that simultaneously guarantees low complexities for dispersal, storage, retrieval, and recovery. At the core of the proposed protocol lies a novel mechanism to encode data in a two-dimensional matrix and a bespoke dispersal algorithm. The protocol maintains an optimal communication complexity for retrieval while substantially improving upon the state of the art for recovery. Additionally, we describe how the protocol can be modified in order to reduce the space complexity and the communication complexity for dispersal, at the expense of a higher communication complexity for retrieval and recovery. As the proposed protocols strike a balance across all considered metrics, they are suitable for a broad range of real-world use cases.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [AdaWidth: Query-Adaptive Embedding Width for Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23862)
*Shubing Yang,Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: AdaWidth是一种自适应维度的稠密检索方法，通过共享前缀表示和正交前缀适配器，根据每个查询的具体需求动态调整评估的嵌入维度数量，在保持检索性能的同时显著减少计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有维度缩减方法要么对所有查询截断相同的维度，要么为每个查询遮蔽不同子集但仍需存储和访问完整嵌入，忽略了不同查询对维度数量的实际需求差异极大这一事实。

Method: AdaWidth包含两个核心组件：一是正交前缀适配器，通过单一学习到的旋转同时作用于查询和文档，将判别性信号集中到前导坐标而保持完整内积不变；二是轻量路由器，通过读取查询已产生的排名的顺序统计量，仅对排名结果可能发生变化的查询评估更多维度。此外还推导了前缀充分性分析，说明了所需维度数量与检索截止处竞争文档的关系。

Result: 在六个检索任务和五个冻结编码器上，AdaWidth以NDCG@10匹配最先进的维度缩减方法的同时，每个查询减少了55%到84%的维度评估。

Conclusion: 所需维度数量随语料库大小对数增长，随检索深度对数减少，且在不同查询间呈重尾分布，这表明按查询自适应分配维度是高效且必要的。

Abstract: High-dimensional embeddings are central to dense retrieval, but not all of these dimensions need to be evaluated at retrieval time. Existing methods reduce dimensions in two ways: truncating the same leading dimensions for every query, or masking a different subset for each query while still storing and accessing the full embedding. Yet queries within a single task differ widely in the number of dimensions they need for their rankings to stabilize.
  We introduce AdaWidth, which adapts the number of evaluated dimensions to each query within a shared prefix representation. An orthogonal prefix adapter applies a single learned rotation to queries and documents alike, concentrating discriminative signal in leading coordinates while leaving every full width inner product unchanged. A lightweight router then reads order statistics off the ranking a query has already produced, and evaluates more dimensions only for the queries whose top results would change. We further derive a prefix sufficiency analysis showing that the required number of dimensions is set by the competing documents at the retrieval cutoff: it grows logarithmically with corpus size, decreases logarithmically with retrieval depth, and remains heavy-tailed across queries. Across six retrieval tasks and five frozen encoders, AdaWidth matches the NDCG@10 of state-of-the-art dimensionality reduction using 55% to 84% fewer dimensions per query.

</details>


### [130] [SQLite is Enough. Lexical, Semantic, and Hybrid Search with scrydb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24060)
*Timo Breuer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了scrydb，一个基于SQLite的Python库，支持词法搜索（利用FTS5全文搜索扩展）、语义搜索（基于sqlite-vec向量搜索扩展）以及两者的混合搜索，通过重排序和结果融合实现轻量级的信息检索解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的信息检索解决方案往往依赖重型基础设施，缺乏轻量级、易集成的替代方案。作者希望利用SQLite的广泛普及性，提供一个结合词法与语义搜索的简洁工具，满足下游检索任务和智能体搜索的需求。

Method: scrydb通过SQLite的FTS5扩展实现词法搜索，通过sqlite-vec扩展实现向量语义搜索，并支持对检索结果进行重排序和分数融合，将两种检索方式结合为混合搜索方案。

Result: 作者在多个信息检索基准数据集上评估了scrydb，证明了其在关键词匹配、语义相似度和排名融合方面进行文本检索的有效性，并提供了查询延迟以及效率与效果之间权衡的分析。

Conclusion: scrydb作为一款采用MIT许可证的开源Python库，为SQLite环境下的信息检索提供了一套轻量、高效且兼具词法与语义能力的解决方案，在效率和效果之间提供了灵活的权衡选择。

Abstract: This work introduces scrydb, a Python library that enables lexical, semantic, and hybrid search within SQLite. For lexical search, scrydb leverages SQLite's full-text search extension FTS5. Semantic search builds on sqlite-vec, a SQLite extension for vector search. Furthermore, the library allows users to rerank and fuse retrieval results to combine both lexical and semantic approaches, providing a lightweight solution for downstream tasks in information retrieval (IR) or agentic search. We evaluate scrydb on various IR benchmark datasets and demonstrate its effectiveness in text retrieval based on keyword matching, semantic similarity, and rank fusion. In addition, we provide insights into query latency and the trade-off between efficiency and effectiveness. scrydb is available under the MIT license.

</details>


### [131] [Wontopos Tablet 2: Measuring Multilingual and Multimodal Memory Retrieval Without Lexical Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23920)
*Sunwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文评估了tablet-2，一个面向语言模型的生产级长期记忆引擎，在文本基准和跨语言无文本照片检索上的表现。系统在LongMemEval-S上得分95.7%，在BEAM-1M上得分67.5%，但作者强调这些数字单独来看意义有限——仅更换阅读器会使分数移动2.0点，更换重问预算会移动8.9点。在多模态跨语言检索中，系统相比BM25和开放稠密基线具有显著优势，语言间跨度更小。论文同时如实报告了三个对系统不利的结果，包括低资源语言性能下降、添加字幕降低检索效果，以及检索管线中某一设置错误导致韩语top-1准确率损失37点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文的动机是评估生产级长期记忆引擎tablet-2在标准文本基准以及跨语言无文本照片检索上的表现，同时强调单一基准分数不足以说明系统能力——需要公平比较，披露对照组设置和不利结果，以避免排名误导。

Method: 系统检索路径不包含词汇匹配、关键词评分或独立的语言模型。作者在两种基准（LongMemEval-S 500题和BEAM-1M 700题/221万条记忆）上测试，并进行多组对照实验：固定引擎/语料/设置/评判者，仅更换读者或重问预算；对BM25进行控制实验；在300张Crossmodal-3600照片、14种语言上进行开放稠密基线比较。

Result: LongMemEval-S得分95.7%，BEAM-1M得分67.5%。仅更换读者移动分数2.0点；仅更换重问预算移动8.9点。对比BM25（19.0% recall@5）达到95.2%，在54个语言单元中BM25完全为零。开放稠密基线语言独立性差（英语91.0%对俄语4.7%）。本系统语言间跨度14.0，远小于基线27.5和27.7。不利结果：斯瓦希里语53.0%、泰卢固语64.0%，添加字幕降低跨语言检索11.4点，一处设置遗漏使韩语top-1损失37点。

Conclusion: 论文结论强调报告应包含配置细节和不利结果，单一基准分数作为"排名"而非"定位"具有误导性。系统在多模态跨语言检索上表现优异，但低资源语言仍是短板，且系统性能对读取器和预算设置敏感。

Abstract: We measure tablet-2, a production long-term memory engine for language models, on the text benchmarks the field already uses and on cross-lingual retrieval of photographs stored with no text at all. Its retrieval path contains no lexical matching, no keyword scoring, and no language model of its own.
  On LongMemEval-S (500 questions) it scores 95.7% [93.4, 97.1]; on BEAM-1M (700 questions, 2.21M stored memories) 67.5% [64.8, 70.2]. Those are question-sampling intervals, not the run-to-run spread, which is an order of magnitude narrower.
  Most of the paper is about how little they mean alone. Holding engine, corpus, settings and judge fixed, changing only the reader moves LongMemEval-S by 2.0 points; changing only the re-ask budget moves BEAM-1M by 8.9. Neither is stated in the reports we compare against, and the second exceeds most gaps there, so we give that table as a placement and not a ranking.
  For the multimodal axis we run two controls. Against BM25, configured as strongly as we could, we reach 95.2% mean recall@5 over 70 store-and-query language cells where BM25 reaches 19.0% and is exactly zero in 54. On captionless photographs a lexical method has no document to score at all. Open dense baselines on 300 Crossmodal-3600 photographs in 14 languages show that density confers no language independence: one scores 91.0% on English and 4.7% on Russian from identical image vectors, and a multilingual variant collapses on Telugu and Swahili. Our spread across languages is 14.0 against their 27.5 and 27.7.
  Three results run against us and are reported at equal weight: low-resource languages degrade sharply (Swahili 53.0%, Telugu 64.0%), attaching captions lowers cross-lingual retrieval by 11.4 points, and one setting omitted into one stage of our own retrieval cost 37 points of Korean top-1 accuracy while leaving nine languages untouched.

</details>


### [132] [Hybrid Semantic Tool Discovery for Enterprise MCP Gateway: Architecture and Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23992)
*Olympia Saha,Amy Wang,Srinivasan Manoharan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SCOUT是一个面向LLM代理的MCP工具选择框架，通过将工具暴露重构为上下文选择问题，仅注入当前步骤相关的工具，使用BM25稀疏检索与稠密向量检索的混合检索（RRF融合）解决上下文饱和和工具发现两大挑战。在PayPal生产中，将MCP工具token消耗从140.2k（上下文70.1%）降至1.3k（0.8%），实现99%的减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP代理服务器聚合大量后端工具，导致两个叠加挑战：一是完整工具schema在用户查询前就使模型上下文窗口饱和（上下文工程瓶颈），二是用户和代理难以在2000+工具中识别最佳工具（工具可发现性障碍）。提示缓存无法解决这些问题。

Method: SCOUT将工具暴露重构为上下文选择问题，仅注入当前步骤相关的工具。它提供两个MCP元工具：tool_search和execute_tool。tool_search执行混合检索，通过Reciprocal Rank Fusion（RRF）融合BM25稀疏匹配与稠密向量搜索，返回top-k相关工具。系统还配备零停机目录更新管道。

Result: 在PayPal生产环境中，SCOUT将MCP工具token消耗从140.2k tokens（占上下文的70.1%）降低到1.3k tokens（占0.8%），实现了99%的减少，大幅降低企业规模的每次查询推理成本。此外，该系统是模型无关的，无需客户端修改。

Conclusion: SCOUT通过将工具暴露视为上下文选择问题，以MCP标准工具的形式实现，有效解决了大型MCP工具生态系统中的上下文饱和和工具发现挑战，在降本增效的同时保持模型无关性和部署便利性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents invoke external tools to retrieve and reason over information beyond pretrained knowledge. The Model Context Protocol (MCP) standardizes how such tools are surfaced, and a proxy MCP server aggregates many backend servers behind a single endpoint providing a secure, governable chokepoint for authentication, policy enforcement, and observability. This architecture creates two compounding challenges: a context-engineering bottleneck where full tool schemas saturate the model context window before any user query, and a tool discoverability barrier where users and agents cannot identify the best tool among 2,000+ indexed tools across 200+ MCP servers. Prompt caching reduces reprocessing cost but neither frees context capacity nor improves accuracy. We present SCOUT (Selective Context Optimization for Universal Tooling), which reframes tool exposure as a context-selection problem, injecting only tools relevant to the current step. SCOUT surfaces two MCP meta-tools -- tool_search and execute_tool -- where tool_search performs hybrid retrieval, fusing BM25 sparse matching with dense vector search via Reciprocal Rank Fusion to return the top-k relevant tools. Backed by zero-downtime catalog update pipelines, SCOUT resolves both context saturation and tool discovery challenges. In production at PayPal, SCOUT reduces MCP tool-token consumption from 140.2k tokens (70.1% of context) to 1.3k tokens (0.8%), a 99% reduction, cutting per-query inference cost at enterprise scale. Because SCOUT is surfaced as standard MCP tools, it is model-agnostic and requires no client-side modifications.

</details>


### [133] [TAGR: Temporally Adaptive Generative Recommendation for Industrial Live-Streaming Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24034)
*Wencai Ye,Guangyi Liu,Chaoyi Wang,Wenbin Luo,Shengyu Wang,Mingjie Sun,Peng Wang,Quanming Yao,Wenjin Wu,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TAGR，一个面向直播广告场景的时间自适应生成式推荐框架，通过在token级（LSID动态刷新语义ID）、意图级（IAG多时间粒度建模）、对齐级（IOPO间歇式在线策略优化）三个层面解决直播广告推荐中静态生成式模型无法适应快速变化内容的问题，在工业平台上线后分别提升进房率8.5%、购物车点击率7.4%、收入16.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直播广告场景中直播内容、推广商品和用户反馈快速变化，对推荐模型有强时效性要求。现有生成式推荐模型为静态域设计，存在三个层面的不足：静态语义ID无法追踪演化的直播广告、单尺度行为建模无法捕捉用户意图转移、偏好优化在新鲜在线反馈与训练稳定性之间存在冲突。

Method: TAGR在三个层面进行时间自适应：(1) token层面：提出Live Semantic-Collaborative ID (LSID)，根据当前直播场景和推广商品周期性刷新每个活跃广告的语义ID，同时保留稳定的分层token词表用于自回归生成；(2) 意图层面：Intent-Aware Generation (IAG) 以多时间粒度的进房历史作为主意图序列，辅助行为单独输入，并用请求后的意图证据和商业价值加权下一token预测损失；(3) 对齐层面：Intermittent On-Policy Preference Optimization (IOPO) 周期性从当前策略采样新鲜候选组，进行行为和价值对齐的偏好更新，并与有监督NTP维护交替进行以保持已学行为分布。

Result: 在大型电商直播广告平台上部署后，TAGR相较生产基线分别提升直播进房率8.5%、购物车点击率7.4%，并实现16.1%的收入提升。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明时间自适应的生成式推荐在直播广告场景中具有显著有效性和工业可行性，验证了在token、意图、对齐三个层面进行动态适应是解决生成式推荐时效性问题的关键路径。

Abstract: Live-streaming advertising is an important monetization channel on short-video and e-commerce platforms, where rapidly changing live content, promoted products, and user feedback impose strong freshness requirements on recommendation models. Existing generative recommenders designed for static domains fail at three levels: static semantic IDs (SID) cannot track evolving live ads; single-scale behavior modeling misses shifting intent; preference optimization conflicts between fresh on-policy feedback and training stability. We propose TAGR, a generative recommendation framework with temporal adaptation at three levels: live-ad tokenization, user intent modeling, and preference alignment. At the token level, Live Semantic-Collaborative ID (LSID) periodically refreshes each active ad's SID based on its current live scene and promoted products, while retaining a stable hierarchical token vocabulary for autoregressive generation. At the intent level, Intent-Aware Generation (IAG) models live-room entry histories at multiple temporal granularities as the primary intent sequence, keeps auxiliary behaviors as separate inputs, and weights next-token prediction (NTP) using post-request intent evidence and business value. At the alignment level, Intermittent On-Policy Preference Optimization (IOPO) periodically samples fresh candidate groups from the current policy and performs behavior- and value-aligned preference updates interleaved with supervised NTP maintenance to preserve learned behavior distribution. Deployed on a large-scale e-commerce live-stream advertising platform, TAGR improves live-room entry and shopping-cart click rates by 8.5% and 7.4%, respectively, and achieves a 16.1% revenue lift over the production baseline. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and industrial viability of temporally adaptive generative recommendation for live-stream advertising.

</details>


### [134] [RetrievalFormer: A Dual-Encoder Transformer for Efficient Approximate Nearest Neighbor Retrieval and Cold-Item Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24079)
*Theodore Rogers,Joe Standerfer,Dmitrii Timoshenko,Haoxue Li,Zuhaib Akhtar,Soyoung Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究共享搜索与推荐索引中"开放性"问题——即新物品仅凭特征即可被检索。研究发现约38.6%的搜索曝光涉及从未展示过的新物品，双编码器检索能保持索引对新物品开放，而ID-softmax推荐器需重训练。虽然全量目录精确训练仍受限于内存，但内容塔在严格冷启动评估中显著优于重训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享搜索与推荐索引必须仅凭特征对新物品打分，因为搜索没有探索槽位来暴露新物品。现有ID-softmax推荐器新物品需要重训练，无法满足该需求。本文旨在量化这种"开放性"的代价与收益。

Method: 在公开日志上验证搜索中新物品出现率（38.6%），对比双编码器、词汇基线与ID-softmax；在MovieLens-1M和MIND上对六个时序基线进行五轮重训练调参比较；修复float32时间戳bug（影响19.7%用户）；实施严格零泄漏冷启动评估，并比较全量softmax与采样InfoNCE训练。

Result: 内容塔在用户冷启动中Recall@20达0.9595（与热启动0.9510相当），冷启动评估达0.172±0.006（1.4倍最强重训练专用方法，3倍无训练基线）。全量softmax比采样训练提升54%（MIND-small）和6.9%（MovieLens-1M），但在240K物品耗尽内存。热启动精度落后最优基线5.2-11.4%（ML-1M），MIND上差距缩小至0.8-3.6%。

Conclusion: 双编码器检索保持索引对新物品开放，无需重训练，代价是热启动精度略有下降，但冷启动表现显著优于专用基线。精确全量softmax训练在目录规模下仍是未解决的核心问题，近似最近邻搜索并非性能瓶颈。

Abstract: A shared search-and-recommendation index must score new items from features alone because search has no exploration slot. In a public log covering both surfaces over one catalog, $38.6\%$ of held-out query-search impressions show an item never previously shown or visited. For user-cold engagements, the feature-based tower serves this demand without measurable loss against $99$ sampled negatives ($0.9595$ Recall@20 versus $0.9510$ warm). A lexical baseline reaches similar parity, while a full-catalog check remains statistically undecided. Dual-encoder retrieval therefore keeps the index \emph{open} to new items, unlike an ID-softmax recommender that requires retraining. We price this openness on recommendation against six sequential baselines, each retrained and tuned through five rounds on corrected targets. A float32 timestamp bug had reordered leave-one-out targets for $19.7\%$ of users. On MovieLens-1M, warm accuracy trails the strongest retrained baseline by $5.2\%$ Recall@20 and $11.4\%$ NDCG@20.
