On MIND, the gap narrows to $0.8$--$3.6\%$ relative to the five strongest baselines, though the model ranks sixth of seven. Under strict zero-leakage cold-start evaluation, the content tower achieves $0.172 \pm 0.006$ Recall@20, $1.4\times$ the strongest retrained dedicated method ($0.124 \pm 0.007$) and $3\times$ a training-free floor, without cold-specific training. Exact full-softmax training raises Recall@20 by $54\%$ on MIND-small and $6.9\%$ on MovieLens-1M over sampled InfoNCE, but recomputes the full catalog each step and exhausts accelerator memory at $240$K items. Approximate nearest-neighbor search explains none of the remaining gap, serving cost does not regress against ID-softmax retrieval, and a history-window sweep explains half the post-recipe remainder. Exact-quality training at catalog scale remains the open problem.

</details>


### [135] [CodeHID: Learning an Addressable Hierarchical Code Index for Generative Code Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24089)
*Zhen Li,Yuhong Chen,Wenhao Xu,Xiaodong Li,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CodeHID，一种生成式代码检索框架，将代码检索从扁平候选匹配重构为从粗到细的语义地址生成。通过伪邻居引导的DocID学习和双阶段DocID生成指导，在CoSQA和ProCQA基准上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码检索模型主要依赖扁平匹配范式，将代码片段视为独立候选，难以区分相似的代码候选。生成式检索虽然通过可学习索引提供了解决方案，但直接应用时标识符空间的前缀不对应于有意义的代码语义区域。

Method: CodeHID包含两个核心组件：1) Pseudo-Neighbor Guided DocID Learning，通过多级残差量化和k近邻伪监督构建全局静态层次索引，确保语义相关的代码片段共享前缀且保持目标级可分离性；2) Dual-Phase DocID Generation Guidance，通过训练阶段的硬负样本和排名蒸馏，以及推理阶段的候选约束和前缀感知解码，可靠地导航固定索引。

Result: 在CoSQA和ProCQA基准上的大量实验表明，CodeHID在大多数情况下以较大幅度超越现有稀疏检索、预训练代码模型、密集代码检索和生成式检索基线，在排名首位检索指标上取得特别强的提升。

Conclusion: CodeHID证明了将代码检索重构为从粗到细的语义地址生成是有效的，层次化索引结合双阶段生成指导能够更好地理解代码语义组织并提升检索性能。

Abstract: Code retrieval models have predominantly relied on a flat matching paradigm that treats code snippets as independent candidates, making them less capable of distinguishing similar code candidates. Generative retrieval offers a solution by constructing a learnable index over the code corpus, guiding the retriever to better understand how code candidates are semantically organized and addressed. However, naively applying generative retrieval in the code retrieval task may result in operating over an identifier space whose prefixes do not correspond to meaningful code-semantic regions. In this paper, we propose CodeHID, a generative code retrieval framework that reformulates the code retrieval task from flat candidate matching to coarse-to-fine semantic address generation. CodeHID relies on two core components. First, Pseudo-Neighbor Guided DocID Learning constructs a globally static hierarchical index by applying multi-level residual quantization and $k$-nearest-neighbor pseudo-supervision, ensuring that semantically related code snippets share prefixes while preserving target-level separability. Second, Dual-Phase DocID Generation Guidance reliably navigates this fixed index by combining training-side ranking enhancements, using hard negatives and rank distillation, with inference-side candidate constraints and prefix-aware decoding. Extensive experiments on CoSQA and ProCQA benchmarks demonstrate that CodeHID outperforms existing sparse retrieval, pre-trained code models, dense code retrieval, and generative retrieval baselines by a large margin in most cases, achieving particularly strong improvements in rank-one retrieval metrics.

</details>


### [136] [Native Multimodal Representation Learning for Click-Through Rate Prediction in E-Commerce Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24091)
*Chao Yi,Feifan Yang,Jiawei Feng,Sishuo Chen,Zhangming Chan,Xiang-Rong Sheng,Han Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Mine-Then-Train方法，通过从CTR数据中挖掘高质量、可被多模态解释的训练样本，用于微调多模态编码器，使其更好地与用户点击偏好对齐，从而解决端到端联合训练无法带来性能提升的问题。离线与在线实验验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态推荐系统采用两阶段范式（预训练多模态编码器+提取特征集成到CTR模型），但多模态预训练任务的目标和数据分布与CTR任务不一致，限制了多模态表示在下游任务中的效果。直接端到端联合训练也未能带来性能提升，因为原始CTR数据中的用户行为同时受多模态语义和非多模态因素驱动，导致监督信号模糊、编码器更新不一致。

Method: 提出Mine-Then-Train方法：首先从CTR数据中挖掘高质量、多模态可解释的训练样本（Mine阶段），然后利用这些样本对多模态编码器进行微调（Train阶段），使其更好地对齐用户点击偏好，最后将微调后的编码器集成到CTR预测模型中。

Result: 离线实验和在线实验均验证了所提出方法的有效性，在CTR预测任务上相比现有方法取得了性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过数据挖掘筛选出多模态可解释的训练样本再微调编码器，可以有效弥合多模态预训练与CTR任务之间的差距，提升多模态表示在下游CTR预测中的效果。

Abstract: Multimodal representations have been widely adopted in industrial e-commerce recommendation systems. Due to their strong semantic understanding and generalization capabilities, they enhance the performance of traditional sparse ID-based Click-Through Rate (CTR) prediction models. Current multimodal application frameworks in the CTR prediction task typically follow a two-stage paradigm: first, pre-training a multimodal encoder on data from specific recommendation scenarios; second, extracting items' multimodal representations using this pre-trained multimodal encoder and integrating them into the CTR prediction model. However, the training objectives and data distribution of multimodal pre-training tasks often differ from those of the CTR prediction task, which limits the effectiveness of multimodal representation on downstream tasks. In this paper, we focus on how to learn Native Multimodal Representation for the CTR prediction task. One intuitive solution is to jointly train the multimodal encoder and CTR model end-to-end on the CTR task, with the expectation that the encoder can automatically learn downstream-relevant knowledge. However, we find that the end-to-end training does not bring performance improvements to existing multimodal application paradigms. Our analysis reveals that user behaviors in raw CTR data are driven by both multimodal semantics and non-multimodal factors, leading to ambiguous supervision and inconsistent encoder updates. To address this, we propose a Mine-Then-Train method that mines high-quality, multimodally interpretable training samples from CTR data and uses them to fine-tune the multimodal encoder for better alignment with user click preferences. Offline and online experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [137] [Tlow: Flow-based Item Tokenizer for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24176)
*Nian Li,Chonggang Song,Jingtao Ding,Lingling Yi,Yong Li,Qingmin Liao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于流的物品分词器Tlow，将语义嵌入变换到统一标准正态分布的潜在空间，实现维度独立和分布简化，从而获得语义清晰的token ID。实验证明其在推荐性能、跨域和多模态推荐上均有显著提升，并在微信大规模多模态检索任务中将用户CTR提升10.32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐模型中使用随机分配的item ID导致参数过多和冷启动问题；RQ-VAE分词器因码本间固有依赖导致解码效率低；而高效的独立分词器（如OPQ）难以处理语义嵌入的维度相关性和分布复杂性。

Method: 提出Tlow（flow-based item Tokenizer），利用标准化流将原始语义嵌入变换到符合标准正态分布的潜在空间，该空间具备维度独立和分布简单的双重优势；在此基础上进行独立分词得到语义清晰的token ID；同时引入码本引导机制，对齐码本空间与token嵌入空间，帮助学习更具语义区分度的token嵌入。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上的离线实验表明Tlow的分词和码本引导显著提升推荐性能，在跨域和多模态推荐上也验证了简化嵌入空间中item分词的有效性。微信大规模多模态检索任务的在线实验显示，基于token ID的检索模型将用户CTR全局提升10.32%，新品项提升11.64%。

Conclusion: Tlow通过流模型实现嵌入空间的简化和独立分词，有效解决了传统分词器在解码效率和维度相关性上的瓶颈，为大规模推荐系统提供了一种高效且语义清晰的item分词方案，并在实际工业场景中验证了其强大的分布变换能力和推荐增益。

Abstract: Item tokenizer encodes semantic embeddings into token IDs to replace the randomly assigned item IDs used in traditional recommendation models, fundamentally addressing the problems of excessive parameters and cold starts. However, the most common tokenizer, RQ-VAE, suffers from low decoding efficiency due to the inherent dependencies among its codebooks. Meanwhile, efficient independent tokenizers such as optimized product quantization (OPQ) still struggle with dimensional correlations and distribution complexity of semantic embeddings. In this work, we propose a f\underline{low}-based item \underline{T}okenizer (Tlow) to transform raw semantic embeddings into a latent space where embeddings conform to a unified standard normal distribution, achieving dual advantages of dimensional independence and distributional simplicity. Independent tokenization performed on these latent embeddings yields semantically clear token IDs. Additionally, we introduce a novel codebook guidance to align the codebook space with the token embedding space, further aiding the learning of more semantically distinct token embeddings. Offline experiments on four public datasets demonstrate that Tlow's tokenization and codebook guidance significantly improve recommendation performance. The improvement on cross-domain and multi-modal recommendations also proves the effectiveness of item tokenization in a simplified embedding space. Online experiments for a multi-modal retrieval task on China's largest social media platform WeChat validate Tlow's powerful distribution transformation capability. The retrieval model based on token IDs improves user CTR by 10.32\% globally and by 11.64\% for new items. Our codes are available at https://github.com/wjjln/Tlow.

</details>


### [138] [RecGPT-Mobile-V2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24295)
*Lingqing Zhang,Bin Zhang,Weipeng Huang,Chengfei Lv,Chengyu Lai,Chuxin Chen,Dimin Wang,Han Zhu,Hongtao Cheng,Jialin Zhu,Jian Wang,Jiuning Lin,Junqing Wu,Li Chen,Qichao Ma,Ruiquan Lan,Shuai Zhong,Tao Wang,Xiaodong Zhu,Yinjiang Cai,Yinnan Song,Yipeng Yu,Yuan Liu,Yuning Jiang,Zhaode Wang,Zhibo Xiao,Zhixin Ma,Zihong Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RecGPT-Mobile-V2，一个端到端框架，将意图质量与执行效率作为耦合目标，通过分阶段设计（证据保留轨迹构建、推荐原生基座适配、分组推理成本优化、教师-学生蒸馏压缩部署）实现个性化查询预测。实验表明证据聚焦的短推理链优于完整五阶段推理，完整奖励公式将查询质量从73.2%提升至78.6%，中位推理链长度从62降至14个token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化查询预测任务在设备端部署面临三大挑战：行为轨迹噪声大且多尺度、单一轨迹可能对应多个有效查询、统一的推理策略要么在简单实例上浪费计算要么在复杂实例上容量不足。现有方法未将意图质量与执行效率作为耦合目标进行联合优化。

Method: 框架包含四个核心环节：（1）将异构交互行为转化为保留证据的轨迹表示；（2）通过领域自适应和监督对齐构建推荐原生基座；（3）仅在分组回滚满足基础效用标准后实施推理成本优化；（4）将教师模型蒸馏为紧凑学生模型，配合低比特执行、结构化压缩和预算感知的设备-云路由部署。

Result: 对齐的思维链消融实验中，证据聚焦的短推理将ROUGE-L从0.228提升到0.315、Jaccard从0.174提升到0.248，且略优于完整五阶段推理。受控强化学习对比中，完整奖励公式将查询质量从73.2%提升到78.6%，硬失败率从3.6%降至1.6%，中位推理链长度从62降到14个token。在线检索分析表明查询召回通道与既有召回通道互补。

Conclusion: 研究支持"充分性导向"而非"均匀简短"的推理策略：应保留决策相关证据，仅在足以提升预测查询质量时分配额外计算资源。这种证据保留与计算分配相结合的方式为设备端个性化查询预测提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Personalized Query prediction maps implicit behavioral signals---clicks, favorites, purchases, and post-purchase exploration---to explicit retrieval intent. On-device deployment makes this task particularly challenging: behavioral trajectories are noisy and multi-scale, multiple Queries may be valid for a single trajectory, and a uniform reasoning policy either expends unnecessary computation on simple instances or allocates insufficient capacity to complex ones. We introduce RecGPT-Mobile-V2, an end-to-end framework that treats intent quality and execution efficiency as coupled objectives within a staged design. The framework transforms heterogeneous interactions into an evidence-preserving trajectory, establishes a recommendation-native foundation through domain adaptation and supervised alignment, and applies reasoning-cost optimization only after grouped rollouts meet grounding and utility criteria. The resulting teacher is distilled into a compact student deployed with low-bit execution, structured compression, and budget-aware device--cloud routing. In an aligned CoT ablation, an evidence-focused short rationale increases ROUGE-L from 0.228 to 0.315 and Jaccard from 0.174 to 0.248, while slightly outperforming the full five-stage rationale. In the controlled RL comparison, the complete reward formulation improves Query quality from 73.2% under quality-only RL to 78.6%, lowers the hard-failure rate from 3.6% to 1.6%, and reduces the median CoT length from 62 to 14 tokens. Online retrieval analysis further indicates that the Query recall channel retrieves inventory complementary to that surfaced by established recall channels. Collectively, these findings support sufficiency-oriented rather than uniformly short reasoning: retain decision-relevant evidence and allocate additional computation only when it is likely to improve the predicted Query.

</details>


### [139] [Rethinking Semantic Alignment in LLM-Enhanced Collaborative Filtering: A Spectral Decoupling Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24363)
*Yedong Jin,Shaowen Peng,Tsunenori Mine,Shoko Wakamiya,Eiji Aramaki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文从谱域视角重新审视LLM增强推荐系统中的表示对齐问题，发现协作信号与语义信号依赖于不同的频谱成分。通过对齐训练会导致表示过度集中于主成分子空间，丢失非主成分语义信息。作者提出UniSpecRec方法，通过信号特定的频谱滤波并保留各表示在各自空间中，无需跨空间对齐或额外参数，直接组合预测结果，取得了优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM增强推荐系统普遍采用将语义表示与协作嵌入在共享空间中对齐的策略，但对齐过程如何影响LLM编码的信息仍不明确。作者希望从频谱角度理解对齐机制的本质缺陷，并据此设计更优的推荐方法。

Method: 作者首先通过分量级评估和训练动态分析揭示对齐将学习表示集中于主导协作子空间和主语义子空间，减少了对非主语义成分的覆盖。基于此观察，提出UniSpecRec，该方法对协作和语义信号分别进行针对性的频谱过滤，保持两者在各自特征空间中，无需跨空间对齐和额外可训练参数，直接在预测层面进行解耦与组合。

Result: 受控实验表明，非主成分在对齐下增益不稳定，但在分量级解耦下能持续提升性能，而完整的预测级解耦取得最佳整体性能。大量实验验证了UniSpecRec的有效性、效率和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了传统对齐策略未能有效利用互补的非主成分语义信息，而通过保留各信号在各自空间并采用预测级解耦的方式，可以更充分地发挥LLM增强推荐的潜力。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM-enhanced recommendation commonly align semantic representations with collaborative embeddings in a shared space, yet how alignment affects LLM-encoded information remains unclear. In this work, we revisit LLM-enhanced recommendation from a spectral perspective and show that collaborative and semantic signals benefit from different spectral parts. While collaborative representations are dominated by smooth low-frequency components due to user-item homophily, semantic embeddings contain useful non-principal singular components. Through component-wise evaluation and training-dynamics analysis, we find that alignment increasingly concentrates learned representations in dominant collaborative and principal semantic subspaces, reducing overlap with non-principal semantic components. Controlled comparisons show that non-principal components provide inconsistent gains under alignment but consistently improve performance through component-level decoupling, while full prediction-level decoupling achieves the best overall performance. These results indicate that alignment fails to effectively exploit complementary non-principal semantic information. Motivated by these findings, we propose UniSpecRec (Unifying Spectral Signals for Recommendation), which applies signal-specific spectral filtering while preserving collaborative and semantic representations in their respective spaces. UniSpecRec combines their predictions without cross-space alignment or additional trainable parameters. Extensive experiments demonstrate its effectiveness, efficiency, and generalizability.

</details>


### [140] [EviGraph: Towards Verifiable Evidence Construction for Information-Seeking Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24667)
*Jiashun Chen,Yirong Mao,Wenhui Que*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: EviGraph是一个将搜索执行与证据记录分离的深度搜索框架，通过共享策略、冻结的证据验证器和确定性结构验证器构建证据图，并利用图的密集过程奖励进行强化学习，在多个基准测试上显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体搜索虽然能检索相关信息，但无法确保检索到的内容真正支撑回答中的论断。线性交互轨迹限制了中间步骤的监督，且优化目标过于聚焦最终答案正确性，缺乏对中间证据构建过程的监督。

Method: EviGraph将搜索执行与证据记录分离：执行器规划简洁查询，冻结的证据验证器检查源页面并返回带显式极性的逐字证据项，共享策略将这些证据映射为添加/支持图请求，由确定性结构验证器检查。证据图既作为持久工作记忆，也作为密集过程奖励的来源，使强化学习直接监督证据构建。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus上，Qwen3-8B EviGraph智能体在匹配交互预算下达到35.9%准确率，而相同双角色架构无强化学习为26.9%，单体智能体仅为2.7%，且生成的token更少。在BrowseComp、GAIA和XBench上也取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 明确结构化并奖励证据记录能够显著提升智能体搜索的表现，验证了将搜索执行与证据记录分离以及利用过程奖励进行监督的有效性。

Abstract: Agentic Web search can retrieve relevant information without establishing that the retrieved content actually supports the claims used in an answer. Existing agents typically keep search and evidence recording in a linear interaction trace and optimize primarily for final-answer correctness, providing limited supervision for intermediate grounding. We present EviGraph, a deep-search framework that separates search execution from evidence recording while using a shared policy for the trainable roles. An executor plans concise queries, a frozen evidence verifier inspects source pages and returns verbatim evidence items with an explicit polarity, and the policy maps those items to add/support graph requests that are checked by a deterministic structural validator. The resulting graph serves both as persistent working memory and as a source of dense process rewards, enabling reinforcement learning to directly supervise evidence construction rather than only the final answer. On BrowseComp-Plus, a Qwen3-8B EviGraph agent achieves 35.9% accuracy under a matched interaction budget, compared with 26.9% for the same dual-role architecture without reinforcement learning and 2.7% for a monolithic agent, while generating fewer tokens per rollout. Consistent gains on BrowseComp, GAIA, and XBench indicate that explicitly structuring and rewarding evidence recording improves agentic search

</details>


### [141] [Auditing Return Conditioning as a Control Knob: An Offline Diagnostic for Decision Transformer Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24815)
*Jingyu Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究离线返回条件（RTG）干预在推荐系统中的可控性问题，发现在MovieLens 25M上当干预覆盖完整上下文时，犯罪类型预测份额提升+23.61个百分点，但仅修改当前token则只提升+1.77个点；打乱RTG的模型大幅消除该响应；在MyAnimeList上则无类似效应。文章提出四项审计检查，结论是跨诊断模式不能确立奖励可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线返回条件干预虽然可以测试推荐系统是否可控，但这种干预很少被审计；以往方法要么重写所有历史RTG token造成越来越合成的上下文，要么只重写当前token更为局部，两种方式的效果未得到系统比较。

Method: 在固定窗口的离线设置下，使用Decision Transformer，通过RTG局部性梯度的实验设计，在MovieLens 25M和MyAnimeList 2020上评估：不同干预范围（K=1到K=20）的RTG重写、无RTG对照、记录匹配与分数奖励检查、以及轨迹内打乱RTG的消融实验。

Result: MovieLens上K=20全上下文干预使犯罪预测份额变化+23.61±2.96个百分点，而仅改当前槽位仅变化+1.77±1.17个点；打乱RTG模型在K=20时只变化+2.08±1.20个点。MAL上K=20使戏剧类型变化-0.03±0.07个点，K=1变化-0.01±0.01个点，均无显著效应。各条件下的类型预测准确率数值接近。

Conclusion: 跨局部性、打乱RTG以及MAL上的零结果的多诊断模式不能确立奖励可控性；由于数据集与焦点类型选择属探索性，数值为描述性的。文章提出四项检查：干预局部性、无RTG基线、奖励检查、RTG内容消融。

Abstract: Offline return-to-go (RTG) sweeps can test whether a recommender conditioned on return is controllable, but the intervention is rarely audited. Rewriting every historical RTG token creates an increasingly synthetic context, while rewriting only the current token is more local. We test this distinction in an offline setting with a fixed window. On MovieLens 25M and MyAnimeList 2020 (MAL), we evaluate a Decision Transformer using an RTG locality ladder, a control without RTG, a logged match and score reward check, and a within-trajectory shuffled RTG ablation. On MovieLens, a $K=20$ intervention that covers the full context, applied only to real context positions, shifts the share of Crime predictions by $+23.61 \pm 2.96$ percentage points from the validation 5th to 95th percentile, whereas changing only the current slot shifts it by $+1.77 \pm 1.17$ points. The shuffled RTG model largely removes this response ($+2.08 \pm 1.20$ points at $K=20$). On MAL, the same protocol does not produce a Drama response: $K=20$ changes Drama by $-0.03 \pm 0.07$ points, and $K=1$ by $-0.01 \pm 0.01$. Genre prediction accuracy is numerically close across real RTG, no RTG, and shuffled RTG, and at $K=1$ logged match rates and matched ratings change little. Because dataset and focus-genre selection were exploratory, these magnitudes are descriptive; the cross-diagnostic pattern across locality, shuffled RTG, and the null result on MAL does not establish reward control. We propose four checks: intervention locality, a no-RTG baseline, a reward check, and RTG-content ablation.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Task-disentangled Low-Rank Adaptation for Versatile Audio-visual Multi-modal Learning Tasks within a Unified Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24209)
*Hanyu Xuan,Mengqi Zhang,Junjun Mao,Fei Wang,Kun Li,Guanghui Yue,Zhiliang Wu,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的音频-视觉多模态学习框架，通过任务解耦的低秩适配（LoRA）机制同时处理多种AVMML任务，包含任务通用低秩矩阵、任务特定调制矩阵和跨任务协作专家三个组件，有效缓解了多任务联合训练中的相互干扰问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AVMML研究大多孤立处理各任务，与人类统一认知能力相悖；而简单的多任务联合训练又因任务间复杂关系导致相互干扰。为此需要一个统一框架来协调多种AVMML任务。

Method: 利用大语言模型的强大表示与泛化能力，设计任务解耦的低秩适配（LoRA）机制。该机制包含三个组件：任务通用低秩矩阵（捕获通用音视频知识）、任务特定调制矩阵（解耦任务特定模式）、跨任务协作专家（利用任务间内在关联），实现从模型和任务两个层面进行显式协作。

Result: 所提方法在多种AVMML任务上超越了现有统一音视频模型，并且在某些任务上还优于大多数任务特定模型。

Conclusion: 通过任务解耦LoRA机制同时从模型和任务两个视角进行显式协作，该方法有效解决了多AVMML任务联合训练中的干扰问题，验证了统一框架处理多任务的有效性。

Abstract: Inspired by human multi-modal perception, Audio-Visual Multi-Modal Learning (AVMML) integrates auditory and visual information to leverage complementary cross-modal cues, enabling more robust and comprehensive scene perception. Existing studies predominantly tackle each AVMML task in isolation, which stands in stark contrast to humans' unified cognitive capacity for handling versatile perception. However, naive joint training across multiple AVMML tasks often suffers from mutual interference, arising from the intricate inter-task relationships. To address this, we propose a unified framework that simultaneously accommodates versatile AVMML tasks. Specifically, benefiting from powerful representation and generalization capabilities of large language models, we design a task-disentangled Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) mechanism that enables dynamic integration of both task-specific and task-shared knowledge, thereby facilitating effective multi-task collaboration. The proposed task-disentangled LoRA comprises three components: a task-general low-rank matrix, task-specific modulation matrices, and cross-task collaboration experts, which respectively capture universal audio-visual knowledge, decouple task-specific pattern, and exploit inherent inter-task correlations. By unifying explicit collaboration from both model and task perspectives, our approach not only surpasses existing unified audio-visual models across multiple AVMML tasks, but also outperforms most task-specific models on certain AVMML tasks.

</details>


### [143] [EVEREST:Endogenous Vision-Language Reinforcement Reasoning Exploration for Urban Socio-Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24640)
*Qixiu Li,Zhongzhi He,Xiang Zhu,Xiaoyong Li,Jiarun Lin,Weifeng Xu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出EVEREST模型，采用以自我为中心的探索策略，主动探测目标边界线索并进行自我修正，同时利用伪代码形式化的自然语言提示来规范执行逻辑，并通过强化学习实现结构化推理，在城市社会语义分割任务中取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市社会语义分割方法依赖被动聚合的全局跨模态线索，缺乏对环境的主动探索，导致目标边界描绘不准确。

Method: 1) 提出EVEREST模型，采用以自我为中心的探索策略（egocentric exploration）主动调查边界线索并自我修正；2) 将离散自然语言提示形式化为伪代码以规范执行逻辑；3) 利用强化学习实现该不可约（irreducible）过程，激发模型的结构化推理能力。

Result: 在真实世界城市社会语义数据集上，EVEREST在所有评估指标上均达到最优性能。

Conclusion: EVEREST模型通过主动探索与自我修正机制，有效解决了现有方法在边界描绘上的缺陷，验证了新探索策略在城市社会语义分割任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Urban socio-semantic segmentation leverages digital and satellite imagery to provide critical spatial semantic information for downstream applications such as urban resource allocation. Although existing methods achieve high segmentation accuracy, they still suffer from inaccurate delineation of target boundaries. The underlying issue is that current models primarily rely on passively aggregated global cross-modal cues, lacking active exploration of the environment. To address this limitation, we propose the EVEREST model, which adopts an egocentric exploration strategy that enables the model to actively investigate boundary cues and perform self-correction. In addition, we formulate discrete natural-language prompts as pseudocode to regularize the execution logic. Reinforcement learning is further employed to implement this irreducible process and elicit the model's structured reasoning capability. Our EVEREST achieves optimal performance on all metrics in the real world urban socio-semantic dataset, demonstrating the superiority of our model. Codes are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/EVEREST-9D21/.

</details>


### [144] [Learning to Prefer Reliably: Error-Augmented Emotion Preference Optimization with Calibrated Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24730)
*Zilong Huang,Junyi Peng,Junjie Li,Kai Li,Wenze Ren,Kong Aik Lee,Man-Wai Mak,Tatsuya Kawahara*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出EAPO框架，通过构造错误增强数据和融合多个MLLM评判器，改进多模态大语言模型在情感偏好判断上的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统成对监督中每个正例只配一个负例，覆盖不足，模型难以学习语义流畅但情感不一致的描述；且单一MLLM评判器存在模型特定偏差。

Method: EAPO框架包含两个层面：数据层面构造错误增强数据集，从每个偏好描述生成多个受控且情感感知的负例；模型层面使用多个独立MLLM评判器，通过边缘校准软融合将异构偏好边缘映射到共同尺度后聚合。

Result: 在MER2026-EmoPrefer挑战数据集和自建错误增强数据集上，EAPO提升了情感偏好预测性能，增强了MLLM评判器在评估与视频多模态情感证据冲突的流畅描述时的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: EAPO通过数据级错误增强和模型级多评判器融合，有效改善了MLLM情感偏好判断的可靠性和鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Emotion preference learning uses pairwise comparisons between candidate descriptions to align multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with human judgments of open-ended emotion descriptions and to train reward models that capture human emotional preferences. However, conventional pairwise supervision is often sparse, typically providing only a single negative description for each positive description, and therefore offers limited coverage of the diverse ways in which an emotion description can be incorrect. In particular, models may be insufficiently exposed to semantically fluent but emotionally inconsistent descriptions. Beyond this data-level limitation, relying on a single MLLM judge introduces a distinct model-level concern: its judgments can be affected by model-specific biases when interpreting fine-grained or ambiguous multimodal emotional cues. To address these limitations, we propose Error-Augmented Preference Optimization (EAPO), a framework for improving the reliability of MLLM-based emotion preference judgment at both the data and model levels. First, we construct an error-augmented dataset by generating multiple controlled and emotion-aware negative descriptions from each preferred description. We then adapt multiple independent MLLM judges to this richer supervision and aggregate their preference margins using margin-calibrated soft fusion, which maps heterogeneous margins to a common scale before aggregation. Experiments on the MER2026-EmoPrefer Challenge dataset and our error-augmented dataset demonstrate that EAPO improves emotion preference prediction and enhances the robustness of MLLM judges when evaluating fluent descriptions that conflict with the video's multimodal emotional evidence. Our code is available at https://github.com/slash1028/EAPO-EmoPrefer.

</details>
